247→    pub trait HyperParamTuner: Send + Sync {\n   248→        /// Produce the next candidate configuration.\n   249→        async fn suggest(&self) -> HashMap<String, HyperParamValue>;\n   250→\n   251→        /// Notify the tuner of the evaluation result so underlying\n   252→        /// strategies can update their priors.\n   253→        async fn record_result(\n   254→            &self,\n   255→            params: HashMap<String, HyperParamValue>,\n   256→            score: f64,\n   257→        ) -> Result<(), TunerError>;\n   258→\n   259→        /// Optional JSON snapshot for 264→    pub struct RandomSearchTuner {\n   265→        specs: HashMap<String, HyperParamSpec>,\n   266→        rng: RwLock<StdRng>,\n   267→    }\n   268→\n   269→    impl RandomSearchTuner {\n   270→        pub fn new(specs: HashMap<String, HyperParamSpec>) -> Self {\n   271→            Self {\n   272→                specs,\n   273→                rng: RwLock::new(StdRng::from_entropy()),\n   274→            }\n   275→        }\n   276→\n   277→        fn sample_from_spec(\n   278→            spec: &HyperParamSpec,\n   279→            rng: &mut StdRng,\n   280→        ) -> HyperParamValue {\n   281→            match spec {\n   282→                HyperParamSpec::IntRange { min, max } => {\n   283→                    let dist = Uniform::new_inclusive(*min, *max);\n   284→                    HyperParamValue::Int(rng.sample(dist))\n   285→                }\n   286→                HyperParamSpec::FloatRange { min, max } => {\n   287→                    let dist = Uniform::new_inclusive(*min, *max);\n   288→                    HyperParamValue::Float(rng.sample(dist))\n   289→                }\n   290→                HyperParamSpec::Choice { options } => {\n   291→                    let idx = rng.gen_range(0..options.len());\n   292→                    HyperParamValue::Text(options[idx].clone())\n   293→                }\n   294→                HyperParamSpec::Flag => HyperParamValue::Flag(rng.gen::<bool>()),\n   295→            }\n   296→        }\n   297→    }\n   298→\n   299→    #[async_trait::async_trait]\n   300→    impl HyperParamTuner for RandomSearchTuner {\n   301→        async fn suggest(&self) -> HashMap<String, HyperParamValue> {\n   302→            let mut rng = self.rng.write().await;\n   303→            self.specs\n   304→                .iter()\n   305→                .map(|(k, spec)| (k.clone(), Self::sample_from_spec(spec, &mut rng)))\n   306→                .collect()\n   307→        }\n   308→\n   309→        async fn record_result(\n   310→            &self,\n   311→            _params: HashMap<String, HyperParamValue>,\n   312→            _score: f64,\n   313→        ) -> Result<(), TunerError> {\n   314→            // Random Search does not learn from feedback.\n   315→            Ok(())\n   316→        }\n   317→\n   318→        async fn snapshot(&self) -> Result<String, TunerError> {\n   319→            serde_json::to_string(&self.specs)\n   320→                .map_err(TunerError::from)\n   321→        }\n   322→    }\n   323→\n   324→    // ------------------------ Factory for Tuners --------------------------\n   325→    pub enum TunerFactory;\n   326→\n   327→    impl TunerFactory {\n   328→        /// Instantiate a tuner from JSON config.\n   329→        pub This module lives in the “Model Ops” layer of VisuTility Orchestrator.\n//! It provides two core capabilities:\n//!  1. Strategy-based hyper-parameter tuning (currently Random Search,\n//!     but additional strategies can be plugged in at runtime).\n//!  2. Thread-safe, observer-friendly experiment tracking that can be\n//!     queried by other layers (e.g. Model Registry, Monitoring).\n//!\n//! Design patterns in play\n//! -----------------------\n//! • Strategy Pattern  – `HyperParamTuner` (trait) + concrete impls.  \n//! • Observer Pattern  – `TrainingEvent` + `TrainingObserver` for model\n//!                       monitoring / automated retraining triggers.\n//! • Factory Pattern   – The `TunerFactory` decides which strategy to\n//!                       instantiate based on runtime config.\n//!\n//! /// Domain-specific errors for hyper-parameter tuning & tracking.\n    #[derive(Debug, Error)]\n    pub enum TunerError {\n        #[error(\"Unknown tuner strategy: {0}\")]\n        UnknownStrategy(String),\n        #[error(\"Experiment run not found: {0}\")]\n        UnknownRun(Uuid),\n        #[error(\"Metric key already exists in this step: {0}\")]\n        DuplicateMetricKey(String),\n        #[error(\"Serialization error: {0}\")]\n        SerdeError(#[from] serde_json::Error),\n        #[error(\"Internal error: {0}\")]\n        Internal(String),\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nmod observer {\n    use super::*;\n\n    /// Observable events emitted by the training loop.\n    #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n    pub enum TrainingEvent {\n        EpochEnd {\n            run_id: Uuid,\n            epoch: usize,\n            metrics: HashMap<String, f64>,\n            timestamp: SystemTime,\n        },\n        TrainingEnd {\n            run_id: Uuid,\n            final_metrics: HashMap<String, f64>,\n            duration: Duration,\n            timestamp: SystemTime,\n        },\n    }\n\n    /// Observer trait that other layers can implement to react to events.\n    pub trait TrainingObserver: Send + Sync {\n        fn on_event(&self, event: &TrainingEvent);\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nmod tracker {\n    use super::*;\n\n    /// Metadata for one experiment run.\n    #[derive(Debug, Clone, #[derive(Clone)]\n    pub struct ExperimentTracker {\n        state: Arc<RwLock<HashMap<Uuid, ExperimentRun>>>,\n        broadcaster: broadcast::Sender<TrainingEvent>,\n    }\n\n    impl ExperimentTracker {\n        pub fn new() -> Self {\n            // 32 is an arbitrary channel size; adjust to throughput needs.\n            let (tx, _) = broadcast::channel(32);\n            Self {\n                state: Arc::new(RwLock::new(HashMap::new())),\n                broadcaster: tx,\n            }\n        }\n\n        /// Subscribe to training events.\n        pub fn subscribe(&self) -> broadcast::Receiver<TrainingEvent> {\n            self.broadcaster.subscribe()\n        }\n\n        /// Start a new run with given parameters. ExperimentRun {\n                run_id: Uuid::new_v4(),\n                params,\n                start_time: SystemTime::now(),\n                end_time: None,\n                metrics: HashMap::new(),\n                tags,\n            };\n            let run_id = run.run_id;\n            self.state.write().await.insert(run_id, run);\n            run_id\n        }\n\n        /// Log metrics for a run & epoch, broadcasting an event.\n        pub async fn log_epoch_metrics(\n            &self,\n            run_id: Uuid,\n            epoch: usize,\n            metrics: HashMap<String, f64>,\n        ) -> Result<(), TunerError> {\n            let mut state = self.state.write().await;\n            let run = state\n                .get_mut(&run_id)\n                .ok_or_else(|| TunerError::UnknownRun(run_id))?;\n\n            // Merge metrics into current map (overwrite duplicates).\n            for (k, v) in &metrics {\n                run.metrics.insert(k.clone(), *v);\n            }\n\n            let event = TrainingEvent::EpochEnd {\n                run_id,\n                epoch,\n                metrics,\n                timestamp: SystemTime::now(),\n            };\n            let _ = self.broadcaster.send(event); // Ignore if no receivers.\n            Ok(())\n        }\n\n        /// Finalize a run, compute its duration & send final event.\n        pub async fn end_run(\n            &self,\n            run_id: Uuid,\n            final_metrics: HashMap<String, f64>,\n        ) -> Result<(), TunerError> {\n            let mut state = self.state.write().await;\n            let run = state\n                .get_mut(&run_id)\n                .ok_or_else(|| TunerError::UnknownRun(run_id))?;\n\n            run.end_time = Some(SystemTime::now());\n            for (k, v) in &final_metrics {\n                run.metrics.insert(k.clone(), *v);\n            }\n\n            let duration = run\n run_id,\n                final_metrics,\n                duration,\n                timestamp: SystemTime::now(),\n            };\n            let _ = self.broadcaster.send(event);\n            Ok(())\n        }\n\n        /// A cheap read-only snapshot of a completed run.\n        pub async fn get_run(&self, run_id: Uuid) -> Option<ExperimentRun> {\n            self.state.read().await.get(&run_id).cloned()\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nmod tuner {\n    use super::*;\n\n    //          match self {\n                HyperParamValue::Int(v) => write!(f, \"{}\", v),\n                HyperParamValue::Float(v) => write!(f, \"{}\", v),\n                HyperParamValue::Text(v) => f64 },\n        Choice { options: Vec<String> },\n        Flag,\n    }\n\n    // -------------------------- Strategy Trait ----------------------------\n    #[async_trait::async_trait]\n    pub trait HyperParamTuner: Send + Sync {\n        /// Produce the next candidate configuration.\n        async fn suggest(&self) -> HashMap<String, HyperParamValue>;\n\n        /// Notify the tuner of the  score: f64,\n        ) -> Result<(), TunerError>;\n\n        /// Optional JSON snapshot for auditability.\n        async fn snapshot(&self) -> Result<String, TunerError>;\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------- Random Search Strategy ------------------------\n    pub struct RandomSearchTuner {\n        specs: HashMap<String, HyperParamSpec>,\n        rng: RwLock<StdRng>,\n    }\n\n    impl RandomSearchTuner {\n        pub fn new(specs: HashMap<String, HyperParamSpec>) -> Self {\n            Self {\n                specs,\n                rng: RwLock::new(StdRng::from_entropy()),\n            }\n        }\n\n        fn sample_from_spec(\n            spec: &HyperParamSpec,\n            rng: &mut StdRng,\n        ) -> HyperParamValue {\n            match spec {\n                HyperParamSpec::IntRange { min, max } => {\n                    let dist = Uniform::new_inclusive(*min, *max);\n                    HyperParamValue::Int(rng.sample(dist))\n                }\n                HyperParamSpec::FloatRange { min, max } => {\n                    let dist = Uniform::new_inclusive(*min, *max);\n                    HyperParamValue::Float(rng.sample(dist))\n                }\n                HyperParamSpec::Choice { options } => {\n                    let idx = rng.gen_range(0..options.len());\n                    HyperParamValue::Text(options[idx].clone())\n                }\n                HyperParamSpec::Flag => HyperParamValue::Flag(rng.gen::<bool>()),\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[async_trait::async_trait]\n    impl HyperParamTuner for RandomSearchTuner {\n        async fn suggest(&self) -> HashMap<String, HyperParamValue> {\n            let mut rng = self.rng.write().await;\n            self.specs\n                .iter()\n                .map(|(k, spec)| (k.clone(), Self::sample_from_spec(spec, &mut rng)))\n                .collect()\n        }\n\n        async fn record_result(\n            &self,\n            _params: HashMap<String, HyperParamValue>,\n            _score: f64,\n        ) -> Result<(), TunerError> {\n            // Random Search does not learn from feedback.\n            Ok(())\n        }\n\n        async fn snapshot(&self) -> Result<String, TunerError> {\n            serde_json::to_string(&self.specs)\n                .map_err(TunerError::from)\n        }\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------ Factory for Tuners --------------------------\n    pub enum TunerFactory;\n\n    impl TunerFactory {\n        /// Instantiate a tuner from JSON config.\n        pub fn Err(TunerError::UnknownStrategy(other.to_string())),\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Example usage (to be Create tracker & subscribe to events\n        let tracker = ExperimentTracker::new();\n        let mut rx = tracker.subscribe();\n\n        // 3. Experiment-tracking and hyper-parameter tuning utilities used by\n//! VisuTility Orchestrator’s Model-Ops layer.\n//!\n//! The module demonstrates a concrete implementation of several of the\n//! architectural patterns mentioned in the project documentation:\n//!   • Strategy Pattern: `HyperParamTuner` selects among tuning strategies\n//!     (`GridSearchTuner`, `RandomSearchTuner`, …).\n//!   • Observer Pattern: `TuningSubject` notifies `TuningObserver`s about new\n//!     trial results so that other subsystems (alerting, dashboards, etc.)\n//!     can react in real-time.\n//!   • Factory Pattern: `TrackerFactory` picks the correct\n//!     `ExperimentTracker` backend.\n//!\n//! JSON-Lines file tracker.\n///\n/// Each trial is appended as a single JSON object on its own line.\npub struct FileTracker {\n    /// Lazily-opened file handle wrapped in a Mutex for thread-safety.\n    file: Arc<Mutex<BufWriter<File>>>,\n}\n\nimpl FileTracker {\n    pub fn new<P: Into<PathBuf>>(path: P) -> Result<Self, OrchestratorError> {\n        let file = OpenOptions::new()\n            .create(true)\n            .append(true)\n            .open(path.into())?;\n        Ok(Self {\n            file: Arc::new(Mutex::new(BufWriter::new(file))),\n        })\n    }\n}\n\n#[async_trait]\nimpl ExperimentTracker for FileTracker {\n    async fn log_trial(&self, trial: &Trial) -> Result<(), OrchestratorError> {\n        let mut writer = self.file.lock().unwrap();\n  Self { tx }\n    }\n\n    pub fn observer_stream(&self) -> broadcast::Receiver<Trial> {\n        self.tx.subscribe()\n    }\n\n    pub fn notify(&self, trial: Trial) -> Result<(), OrchestratorError> &self,\n    ) -> Vec<HashMap<String, HyperParamValue>> {\n        fn helper(\n            keys: &[String],\n            idx: usize,\n            grid: &HashMap<String, Vec<HyperParamValue>>,\n            acc: &mut HashMap<String, HyperParamValue>,\n            out: &mut Vec<HashMap<String, HyperParamValue>>,\n        ) {\n            if idx == keys.len() {\n                out.push(acc.clone());\n ExperimentTracker: Send + Sync {\n    fn log_trial(&self, evt: &TrialFinishedEvent) -> Result<()>;\n}\n\n/// Tracker that appends each trial as JSON to a local file.\n/// Suitable for on-prem installations without an external tracking service.\npub struct LocalFileTracker {\n    file: RwLock<BufWriter<File>>,\n}\n\nimpl LocalFileTracker {\n    pub fn open<P: AsRef<Path>>(path: P) -> Result<Self> {\n        let file = OpenOptions::new()\n            .create(true)\n            .append(true)\n            .open(path)?;\n        Ok(Self {\n            file: RwLock::new(BufWriter::new(file)),\n        })\n    }\n}\n\nimpl ExperimentTracker for LocalFileTracker {\n    fn log_trial(&self, evt: &TrialFinishedEvent) -> Result<()> {\n        let mut writer = ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n/// Glue-code tying everything together.\n/// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\npub struct HyperparameterRunner {\n    tuner: Box<dyn Tuner>,\n    bus: Arc<EventBus>,\n    trackers: Vec<Arc<dyn ExperimentTracker>>,\n}\n\nimpl HyperparameterRunner {\n    pub fn new(\n        tuner: Box<dyn Tuner>,\n        bus: Arc<EventBus>,\n        trackers: Vec<Arc<dyn ExperimentTracker>>,\n    ) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            tuner,\n            bus,\n            trackers,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Blocking loop that pulls a candidate, executes user-provided evaluation\n    /// closure, and then broadcasts / logs the result.\n    pub fn run<Evaluate>(&mut self, mut eval_fn: Evaluate) -> Result<()>\n    where\n        Evaluate: FnMut(&Candidate) -> Result<f64>,\n    {\n        loop {\n            let cand = match self.tuner.next_candidate() {\n                Ok(c) => c,\n                Err(TuningError::ExhaustedSpace) => break,\n                Err(e) => return Err(e),\n            };\n\n            let score = eval_fn(&cand)?;\n            self.tuner.update_result(&cand, score)?;\n\n            let evt = TrialFinishedEvent {\n                candidate: cand,\n                score,\n                finished_at: Utc::now(),\n            };\n\n            // Send to event bus\n            self.bus.dispatch(evt.clone());\n\n            // Persist via trackers\n            for t in &self.trackers {\n                if let Err(e) = t.log_trial(&evt) {\n                    // non-fatal: log and keep going\n                    log::warn!(\"Experiment-tracker error: {e}\");\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n\n/// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n/// Convenience hash implementation for Candidate using `fxhash` for speed.\n/// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\nmod fxhash {\n    #![allow(clippy::unseparated_literal_suffix)]\n    // 64-bit Fowler–Noll–Vo hash adapted for quick & dirty deduplication.\n    pub fn hash64(candidate: &super::Candidate) -> u64 {\n        let mut hash: u64 = 0xcbf29ce484222325;\n        for (k, v) in candidate {\n            for byte in k.as_bytes().iter().chain(v.to_string().as_bytes()) {\n                hash ^= *byte as u64;\n                hash = hash.wrapping_mul(0x00000100000001b3);\n            }\n        }\n        hash\n    }\n}\n\n/// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n/// Unit tests\n/// fn from(v: f64) -> Self {\n            ParamValue::Float(v)\n        }\n    }\n    impl From<i64> for ParamValue {\n        fn from(v: i64) -> Self {\n            ParamValue::Int(v)\n        }\n    61→/// product of discrete candidate values.\n    62→///\n    63→/// Useful for deterministic sweeps when the search space is small.\n    64→pub struct GridSearchTuner {\n    65→    grid:            &candidates.into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>(),\n    77→            0,\n    78→        );\n    79→        info!(\"GridSearch initialized with {} configurations\", grid.len());\n    80→ &[(String, Vec<f64>)],\n    87→        idx: usize,\n    88→    ) {\n    89→        if idx == items.len() {\n    90→            acc.push(current.clone());\n    91→            return;\n    92→ That responsibility\n/// lies with the `Model Ops` layer.  The tuner is also side-effect free––\n/// apart from internal bookkeeping.\npub trait HyperparameterTuner: Send + Sync {\n    /// Suggest the next configuration to evaluate.\n    fn suggest(&mut self) -> Option<HyperParams>;\n\n    /// Report the evaluation metric (e.g. accuracy, loss) for a previously\n    /// suggested configuration.  Implementations may choose to ignore the\n    /// score when the configuration is unknown.\n    fn report_result(&mut self, params: &HyperParams, metric: f64);\n\n    /// Human-readable name; useful for telemetry & debugging.\n    fn name(&self) -> &'static str;\n}\n\n/// A simple grid-search tuner that exhaustively evaluates the Cartesian\n/// product of discrete candidate values.\n///\n/// Useful for deterministic sweeps when the search space is small.\npub struct GridSearchTuner {\n    grid: Vec<HyperParams>,\n    cursor: usize,\n}\n\nimpl GridSearchTuner {\n    pub fn new(candidates: HashMap<String, Vec<f64>>) -> Self {\n        // Compute Cartesian product\n        let mut grid: Vec<HyperParams> = vec![];\n        Self::cartesian_product(\n            &mut grid,\n            &mut HyperParams::new(),\n            &candidates.into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>(),\n            0,\n        );\n        info!(\"GridSearch initialized with {} configurations\", grid.len());\n        Self { grid, cursor: 0 }\n    }\n\n    fn cartesian_product(\n        acc: &mut Vec<HyperParams>,\n        current: &mut HyperParams,\n        items: &[(String, Vec<f64>)],\n        idx: usize,\n    ) {\n        if idx == items.len() {\n            acc.push(current.clone());\n            return;\n        }\n\n        let (ref key, ref values) = items[idx];\n        for v in values {\n            current.insert(key.clone(), *v);\n            Self::cartesian_product(acc, current, items, idx + 1);\n        }\n        current.remove(key);\n    }\n}\n\nimpl HyperparameterTuner for GridSearchTuner {\n    fn suggest(&mut self) -> Option<HyperParams> {\n        if self.cursor >= self.grid.len() {\n            None\n        } else {\n            let params = self.grid[self.cursor].clone();\n            self.cursor += 1;\n            Some(params)\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn report_result(&mut self, _params: &HyperParams, _metric: f64) {\n        // grid search is stateless; nothing to do.\n    }\n\n    fn name(&self) -> &'static str {\n        \"GridSearchTuner\"\n    }\n}\n\n/// Random search tuner that samples uniformly from user-supplied ranges.\n/// Each range is inclusive.\n///\n/// NOTE: For continuous variables, the range is treated as continuous;\n/// for discrete variables, the caller can pass equal min==max to achieve\n/// deterministic behavior.\npub struct RandomSearchTuner {\n    ranges: HashMap<String, (f64, f64)>,\n    max_trials: usize,\n    trials: usize,\n    history: Vec<(HyperParams, f64)>,\n}\n\nimpl RandomSearchTuner {\n    pub fn new(ranges: HashMap<String, (f64, f64)>, max_trials: usize) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            ranges,\n            max_trials,\n            trials: 0,\n            history: Vec::new(),\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn random_sample(&self) -> HyperParams {\n        let mut rng = thread_rng();\n        self.ranges\n            .iter()\n            .map(|(k, (min, max))| (k.clone(), rng.gen_range(*min..=*max)))\n            .collect()\n    }\n}\n\nimpl HyperparameterTuner for RandomSearchTuner {\n    fn suggest(&mut self) -> Option<HyperParams> {\n        if self.trials >= self.max_trials {\n            None\n        } else {\n            self.trials += 1;\n            Some(self.random_sample())\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn report_result(&mut self, params: &HyperParams, metric: f64) {\n        self.history.push((params.clone(), metric));\n    }\n\n    fn name(&self) -> &'static str {\n        \"RandomSearchTuner\"\n    }\n}\n\n/// Factory pattern: build tuners dynamically based on a string identifier.\n/// This allows runtime configuration via CLI, YAML, or remote control plane.\npub enum TunerFactory;\n\nimpl TunerFactory {\n    pub fn make_grid_search(candidates: HashMap<String, Vec<f64>>) -> Box<dyn HyperparameterTuner> {\n        Box::new(GridSearchTuner::new(candidates))\n    }\n\n    pub fn make_random_search(\n        ranges: HashMap<String, (f64, f64)>,\n        max_trials: usize,\n    ) -> Box<dyn HyperparameterTuner> {\n        Box::new(RandomSearchTuner::new(ranges, max_trials))\n    }\n}\n\n/// Observer pattern – The model-training engine emits `TrainingEvent`s that\n/// observers can subscribe to.  The tuner implementation itself can be an\n/// observer in order to implement adaptive search algorithms such as Bayesian\n/// optimization, however, we keep them decoupled so that multiple observers\n/// (e.g. loggers, alerting) can co-exist.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub enum TrainingEvent {\n    EpochStart { epoch: u32 },\n    EpochEnd {\n        epoch: u32,\n        metrics: HashMap<String, f64>,\n    },\n    TrainingFinish {\n        best_metric: f64,\n        best_params: HyperParams,\n    },\n}\n\n/// Observer trait.\npub trait TrainingObserver: Send + Sync {\n    fn on_event(&self, event: &TrainingEvent);\n}\n\n/// Default implementation that feeds back the final metric to the tuner.\npub struct TunerObserver {\n    tuner: Arc<Mutex<Box<dyn HyperparameterTuner>>>,\n}\n\nimpl TunerObserver {\n    pub fn new(tuner: Arc<Mutex<Box<dyn HyperparameterTuner>>>) -> Self {\n        Self { tuner }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl TrainingObserver for TunerObserver {\n    fn on_event(&self, event: &TrainingEvent) {\n        if let TrainingEvent::TrainingFinish {\n            best_metric,\n            best_params,\n        } = event\n        {\n observer\");\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Lightweight experiment tracking backed by SQLite.\n/// Production deployments can replace this with a more\n/// sophisticated store (Postgres, ExperimentTracker {\n    /// Create a new tracker with the given file path.\n    pub fn new<P: AsRef<Path>>(db_path: P) -> Result<Self> {\n        let conn = Connection::open(&db_path)\n            .with_context(|| format!(\"opening experiments db {:?}\", db_path.as_ref()))?;\n\n        let tracker = Self {\n            db_path: db_path.as_ref().to_path_buf(),\n            conn,\n        };\n\n hyperparams\")?;\n\n        self.conn.execute(\n            \"\n            INSERT INTO experiments (timestamp, tuner_name, params, metric)\n            VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4);\n            \",\n            params![\n                timestamp.to_rfc3339(),\n                tuner_name,\n                params_blob,\n                metric\n            ],\n        )?;\n\n        Ok(self.conn.last_insert_rowid())\n    }\n\n    /// Retrieve all experiments for auditing or analytics.\n    pub fn all_records(&self) -> Result<Vec<ExperimentRecord>> {\n        let mut stmt = self.conn.prepare(\n            \"\n            SELECT id, timestamp, tuner_name, params, metric\n            FROM experiments\n            ORDER BY id ASC;\n            \",\n        )?;\n\n        let iter = stmt.query_map([], |row| {\n            let params_blob: Vec<u8> = row.get(3)?;\n            let params: HyperParams = serde_json::from_slice(&params_blob)\n                .map_err(|e| rusqlite::Error::FromSqlConversionFailure(3, rusqlite::types::Type::Blob, Box::new(e)))?;\n\n            Ok(ExperimentRecord {\n                id: row.get(0)?,\n                timestamp: rusqlite::types::Type::Text, Box::new(e)))?,\n                tuner_name: row.get(2)?,\n                params,\n                metric: row.get(4)?,\n            })\n        })?;\n\n        let mut out = Vec::new();\n        for rec in iter {\n            out.push(rec?);\n        }\n        Ok(out)\n    }\n}\n\n/// Example of orchestrating an end-to-end hyper-parameter tuning session.\n/// The implementation for higher-level orchestration code.\npub fn run_hpo_session() -> Result<()> {\n    // ------------------------------------------\n    // Create the tuner (here: grid search)\n    // ------------------------------------------\n    let mut candidates: HashMap<String, Vec<f64>> = HashMap::new();\n    candidates.insert(\"learning_rate\".into(), vec![0.01, 0.001]);\n    candidates.insert(\"dropout\".into(), vec![0.1, 0.2, 0.3]);\n\n    let tuner: Arc<Mutex<Box<dyn HyperparameterTuner>>> =\n        Arc::new(Mutex::new(TunerFactory::make_grid_search(candidates)));\n\n    // ------------------------------------------\n    // Initialize experiment tracker\n    // ------------------------------------------\n    let tracker = info!(\"Hyper-parameter search complete.\");\n            break;\n        };\n\n        // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n        // >>>>  In the real system, .context(\"logging experiment\")?;\n\n        debug!(\n            \"Evaluated {:?} -> metric = {:.4}\",\n            &params, simulated_metric\n        );\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod (\"batch_size\".to_string(), (16.0, 128.0)),\n        ]);\n\n        let mut tuner = RandomSearchTuner::new(ranges, 5);\n        let p1 = tuner.suggest().unwrap();\n        tuner.report_result(&p1, VisuTility Orchestrator – Model Monitoring & Automated Retraining\n//! -----------------------------------------------------------------\n//! This module brings together several architectural patterns\n//! (Observer, Strategy, Factory) to deliver production-grade,\n//! concurrent model monitoring and automated retraining.\n//!\n//! High-level responsibilities\n//! • Collect structured model-performance events\n//! • Notify interested observers in a thread-safe fashion\n//! • Decide—based on configurable policies—whether a model\n//!   should be retrained\n//! • Execute hyper-parameter tuning experiments using a pluggable\n//!   strategy pattern\n//! • Persist experiment metadata for lineage & auditability\n//!\n//! In real deployments this module plugs into other layers such as\n//! the Model Registry, Feature-Ops, and Serving-Ops, but remains\n//! fully self-contained here for demonstration purposes.\n\nuse std::{\n    collections::HashMap,\n    path::{Path, PathBuf},\n    sync::{Arc, Mutex, RwLock},\n    time::Duration,\n};\n\nuse chrono::{DateTime, Utc};\nuse rand::{distributions::Uniform, Rng};\nuse entire module\n#[derive(Debug, Error)]\npub enum MonitorError {\n    #[error(\"IPC channel disconnected\")]\n    ChannelDisconnected,\n    #[error(\"Experiment failed: {0}\")]\n    ExperimentFailed(String),\n    #[error(\"Hyper-parameter tuning error: {0}\")]\n    HyperparamTuning(String),\n    #[error(\"IO error: {0}\")]\n    Io(#[from] std::io::Error),\n    #[error(\"Serialization error: {0}\")]\n    Serialization(#[from] serde_json::Error),\n    #[error(\"Internal mutex poisioned\")]\n    Poisoned,\n}\n\n/// A single snapshot of model-performance metrics.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct ModelMetrics {\n    pub model_name: String,\n    pub version: String,\n    pub accuracy: f32,\n    pub precision: f32,\n    pub recall: f32,\n    pub timestamp: DateTime<Utc>,\n}\n\n/// The event that flows through the Observer pipeline.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub enum ModelEvent {\n    Metrics(ModelMetrics),\n    // In a real system additional event variants would live here, e.g.\n    // SchemaDriftDetected, DataQualityIssue, etc.\n}\n\n/// Observer pattern: who listens to events?\npub trait Observer: Send + Sync + 'static {\n    fn on_event(&self, event: &ModelEvent) -> Result<(), MonitorError>;\n}\n\n/// Observer pattern: who emits events?\npub trait Subject: Send + Sync + 'static {\n    fn register_observer(&mut self, obs: Arc<dyn Observer>);\n    fn deregister_observer(&mut self, obs_id: usize);\n    fn notify(&self, event: ModelEvent);\n}\n\n/// Thread-safe, async model-monitor implementation.\npub struct ModelMonitor {\n    observers: RwLock<Vec<Arc<dyn Observer>>>,\n    tx: UnboundedSender<ModelEvent>,\n}\n\nimpl ModelMonitor {\n    pub fn new(buffer: usize) -> Arc<Self> {\n        let (tx, rx) = mpsc::unbounded_channel();\n        let monitor = Arc::new(Self {\n            observers: RwLock::new(Vec::new()),\n            tx,\n        });\n\n        // Spawn async dispatcher\n        ModelMonitor::spawn_dispatch_loop(Arc::clone(&monitor), rx, buffer);\n\n        monitor\n    }\n\n    /// Non-blocking send of new events\n    pub fn submit_event(&self, event: ModelEvent) -> Result<(), MonitorError> {\n        self.tx\n            .send(event)\n            .map_err(|_| MonitorError::ChannelDisconnected)\n    }\n\n    fn spawn_dispatch_loop(\n        monitor: Arc<ModelMonitor>,\n        mut rx: UnboundedReceiver<ModelEvent>,\n        _buffer: usize,\n    ) {\n        task::spawn(async move {\n            while let Some(event) = rx.recv().await {\n                monitor.notify(event);\n            }\n        });\n    }\n}\n\nimpl Subject for ModelMonitor {\n    fn register_observer(&mut self, obs: Arc<dyn Observer>) {\n        let mut guard = self\n            .observers\n            .write()\n            .expect(\"ModelMonitor observer lock poisoned\");\n        guard.push(obs);\n    }\n\n    fn deregister_observer(&mut self, obs_id: usize) {\n        let mut guard = self\n            .observers\n            .write()\n            .expect(\"ModelMonitor observer lock poisoned\");\n        if obs_id < guard.len() {\n            guard.remove(obs_id);\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn notify(&self, event: ModelEvent) {\n        let guard = self\n            .observers\n            .read()\n            .expect(\"ModelMonitor observer lock poisoned\");\n        for obs in guard.iter() {\n            // Fire & forget; each observer must handle its own failure modes\n            let evt = event.clone();\n            let obs = Arc::clone(obs);\n            task::spawn(async move {\n                if let Err(e) = obs.on_event(&evt) {\n                    eprintln!(\"Observer failed to handle event: {e}\");\n                }\n            });\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Hyper-parameter tuning strategy pattern\npub trait HyperparamTuningStrategy: Send + Sync + 'static {\n    fn RandomSearchStrategy {\n    pub max_trials: usize,\n}\n\nimpl HyperparamTuningStrategy for RandomSearchStrategy {\n    fn tune(\n        &self,\n        experiment_id: &str,\n        _dataset_path: &Path,\n    ) -> Result<HyperparamSet, MonitorError> {\n        println!(\n            \"RandomSearchStrategy: starting experiment {experiment_id} ({} trials)\",\n            self.max_trials\n        );\n\n        let mut rng = rand::thread_rng();\n        let range = Uniform::new(0.0, 1.0);\n        let mut best_score = 0.0;\n        let mut best_params = HashMap::new();\n\n        for trial in 0..self.max_trials {\n            let lr = rng.sample(range);     // learning rate\n            let dropout = rng.sample(range); // dropout\n            let score = rng.sample(range);   // pretend validation accuracy\n\n            println!(\n                \"Trial {trial}: lr={lr:.4}, dropout={dropout:.2} => val_acc={score:.3}\"\n            );\n\n            if score > best_score {\n                best_score = score;\n                best_params.insert(\"learning_rate\".into(), lr.to_string());\n                best_params.insert(\"dropout\".into(), dropout.to_string());\n            }\n        }\n\n        Ok(HyperparamSet {\n            params: best_params,\n            score: best_score,\n        })\n    }\n}\n\n/// A set of tuned hyper-parameters and its validation score\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct HyperparamSet {\n    pub params: HashMap<String, String>,\n    pub score: f32,\n}\n\n/// Simplified experiment tracker\npub struct    log_path: log_path.into(),\n            inner: Mutex::new(()),\n        }\n    }\n\n    pub fn record(\n        &self,\n        experiment_id: &str,\n        hyperparams: &HyperparamSet,\n    ) ->            .create(true)\n            .append(true)\n            .open(&self.log_path)?\n            .write_all(format!(\"{experiment_id},{json}\\n\").as_bytes())?;\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n\n/// HyperparamTuningStrategy>,\n    pub tracker: Arc<ExperimentTracker>,\n    pub dataset_path: PathBuf,\n}\n\nimpl Observer for RetrainTrigger {\n    fn on_event(&self, event: &ModelEvent) -> Result<(), MonitorError> {\n        match event {\n            ModelEvent::Metrics(metrics) => {\n                if metrics.accuracy < self.min_accuracy {\n                    println!(\n                        \"RetrainTrigger: accuracy {:.3} below threshold {:.3}\",\n                        metrics.accuracy, self.min_accuracy\n                    );\n                    let experiment_id =\n                        format!(\"{}_{}_{:x}\", metrics.model_name, metrics.version, rand::random::<u32>());\n                    let tuner = Arc::clone(&self.tuner);\n                    let tracker = Arc::clone(&self.tracker);\n                    let dataset = self.dataset_path.clone();\n\n                    // Async retraining so we don't block monitoring loop\n                    task::spawn(async move {\n                        if let Err(e) =\n                            run_retraining(experiment_id, tuner, tracker, dataset).await\n                        {\n                            eprintln!(\"Retraining failed: {e}\");\n                        }\n                    });\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n\n/// Core retraining routine\nasync fn run_retraining(\n    experiment_id: String,\n    tuner: Arc<dyn HyperparamTuningStrategy>,\n    tracker: Arc<ExperimentTracker>,\n    dataset: PathBuf,\n) -> Result<(), MonitorError> {\n    println!(\"Starting retraining experiment {experiment_id}\");\n\n    let tuned = tuner.tune(&experiment_id, &dataset)?;\n    tracker.record(&experiment_id, &tuned)?;\n\n    // Simulate asynchronous model training\n    println!(\n        \"Training model with best val_acc={:.3} and params={:?}\",\n        tuned.score, tuned.params\n    );\n    sleep(Duration::from_secs(2)).await; // pretend training time\n\n    println!(\"Experiment {experiment_id} completed successfully\");\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n// Module 55 ­— Hyper-parameter Tuning & Experiment Tracking\n//! ---------------------------------------------------------\n//! This module contains the implementation of the Strategy‐based hyper-parameter\n//! tuner as well as a lightweight Observer for experiment tracking.  Although\n//! the production project offers many more knobs, this file provides a realistic\n//! slice that can be embedded directly into the VisuTility Orchestrator.\n//!\n//! Architectural Patterns Demonstrated\n//! * Strategy Pattern    – `TunerStrategy` trait + concrete tuners\n//! * Factory  Pattern    – `TunerFactory` for late-bound instantiation\n//! * Observer Pattern    – `ExperimentEvent`  + `ExperimentObserver`\n//! * Pipeline  Pattern    – `run_hyperparameter_tuning` async pipeline\n//!\n//! External Crates\n//! * `rand`              – random search strategy\n//! * `tokio`             – async execution\n//! * `serde`, `serde_json` – (De)serialising trial metadata\n//! * `thiserror`         – ergonomic error definitions\n\n// --------------------------- Dependency Imports ------------------------------\nuse rand::{rngs::StdRng, seq::IteratorRandom, Rng, SeedableRng};\nuse ---------------------------- Error Definition -------------------------------\n#[derive(Debug, Error)]\npub enum TuningError {\n    #[error(\"model training failed: {0}\")]\n    TrainFailure(String),\n    #[error(\"invalid hyper-parameter space: {0}\")]\n    InvalidSpace(String),\n    #[error(\"internal error: {0}\")]\n    Internal(String),\n}\n\n// ------------------------- Trainable Model Abstraction -----------------------\n/// A minimal contract that every model inside VisuTility needs to implement\n/// in order to participate in automated tuning.\npub trait Trainable: Send + Sync + Debug + 'static {\n    /// User-level hyper-parameter type.\n    type HyperParams: Serialize + for<'de> Deserialize<'de> + Send + Sync + Clone + Debug;\n    /// Metric returned by evaluation.\n    type Metric: PartialOrd + Copy + Debug;\n\n    /// Create a new model instance with the supplied hyper-parameters.\n    fn new(params: &Self::HyperParams) -> Result<Self, TuningError>\n    where\n        Self: Sized;\n\n    /// Train the model on given data split.\n    fn train(&mut self, dataset: DatasetView) -> Result<(), TuningError>;\n\n    /// Validate the model and return a metric (higher is better).\n    fn validate(&self, dataset: DatasetView) -> Result<Self::Metric, TuningError>;\n}\n\n// ----------------------------- Data Contracts --------------------------------\n/// A lightweight view over a data split.\n///\n/// NOTE: In the real platform this is a zero-copy view into an Arrow Flight\n/// stream.  For brevity, we merely keep a string ID here.\n#[derive(Clone, Debug)]\npub struct DatasetView {\n    pub id: String,\n}\n\n// ------------------------------ Experiment I/O -------------------------------\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct TrialRecord<M: Trainable> {\n    pub params: M::HyperParams,\n    pub metric: M::Metric,\n    pub TrialCompleted(TrialRecord<M>),\n    TuningFinished(Option<TrialRecord<M>>),\n}\n\n/// Observer interface (pull-based is also supported in other modules).\npub trait ExperimentObserver<M: Trainable>: Send + Sync {\n    fn on_event(&self, event: ExperimentEvent<M>);\n}\n\n/// Thread-safe, in-memory observer used in tests and local runs.\n#[derive(Default)]\npub struct InMemoryObserver<M: Trainable> {\n    events: Arc<Mutex<Vec<ExperimentEvent<M>>>>,\n}\nimpl<M: Trainable> InMemoryObserver<M> {\n    pub fn events(&self) -> Vec<ExperimentEvent<M>> {\n        self.events.lock().unwrap().clone()\n    }\n}\nimpl<M: Trainable> ExperimentObserver<M> for InMemoryObserver<M> {\n    fn on_event(&self, event: ExperimentEvent<M>) {\n        self.events.lock().unwrap().push(event);\n    }\n}\n\n// ----------------------------- Tuner Strategy --------------------------------\npub trait TunerStrategy<M: Trainable>: Send + Sync {\n    /// Run the tuning loop and return the best trial record (if TuningError>;\n}\n\n/// Hyper-parameter search space.\n///\n/// In the real system this would allow conditional spaces, distributions,\n/// nested structures, etc.  Here we simply map parameter names to candidate\n/// categorical values.\n#[derive(Clone)]\npub struct HyperParamSpace<M: Trainable> {\n    choices: HashMap<String, Vec<serde_json::Value>>,\n    _phantom: std::marker::PhantomData<M>,\n}\nimpl<M: Trainable> HyperParamSpace<M> {\n    pub fn new() -> Self {\n        Self {\n            choices: HashMap::new(),\n            _phantom: std::marker::PhantomData,\n        }\n    }\n\n    pub fn add_choice<T: Serialize>(&mut self, name: &str, values: Vec<T>) {\n        self.choices.insert(\n            name.to_owned(),\n            values\n                .into_iter()\n                .map(|v| serde_json::to_value(v).expect(\"serialise hyper-param\"))\n                .collect(),\n        );\n    }\n\n    fn enumerate_grid(&self) -> Vec<M::HyperParams> {\n        // A naïve Cartesian product enumerator. Usually replaced by an iterator\n        // that yields lazily to avoid combinatorial explosion.\n        let mut param_maps: Vec<HashMap<String, serde_json::Value>> = vec![HashMap::new()];\n\n        for (key, choices) in &self.choices {\n            let mut next_maps = Vec::new();\n            for choice in choices {\n                for base in &param_maps {\n                    let mut map = base.clone();\n                    map.insert(key.clone(), choice.clone());\n                    next_maps.push(map);\n                }\n            }\n            param_maps = next_maps;\n        }\n\n        param_maps\n            .into_iter()\n            .map(|map| serde_json::from_value::<M::HyperParams>(serde_json::Value::Object(\n                map.into_iter().collect(),\n            ))\n            .expect(\"convert to HyperParams\"))\n            .collect()\n    }\n\n    fn pick_random(&self, rng: &mut StdRng) -> Option<M::HyperParams> {\n        let mut map = HashMap::<String, serde_json::Value>::new();\n        for (k, choices) in &self.choices {\n            let choice = choices.iter().choose(rng)?;\n            map.insert(k.clone(), choice.clone());\n        }\n        serde_json::from_value(serde_json::Value::Object(map.into_iter().collect())).ok()\n    }\n}\n\n// --------------------- Concrete Strategies ----------------------------------\npub struct GridSearchTuner;\nimpl<M: Trainable> TunerStrategy<M> for GridSearchTuner {\n    fn tune(\n        &self,\n        space: HyperParamSpace<M>,\n        dataset: DatasetView,\n        observers: &[Arc<dyn ExperimentObserver<M>>],\n    ) -> Result<Option<TrialRecord<M>>, TuningError> {\n        let mut best: Option<TrialRecord<M>> = None;\n\n        for params in space.enumerate_grid() {\n            notify(observers, ExperimentEvent::TrialStarted(params.clone()));\n            let start = Instant::now();\n\n            let mut model = M::new(&params)?;\n            model.train(dataset.clone())?;\n            let metric = model.validate(dataset.clone())?;\n\n            let record = TrialRecord {\n                params: params.clone(),\n                metric,\n                duration_ms: start.elapsed().as_millis(),\n            };\n            notify(observers, ExperimentEvent::TrialCompleted(record.clone()));\n\n            if best.as_ref().map(|b| metric > b.metric).unwrap_or(true) {\n                best = Some(record);\n            }\n        }\n\n        notify(observers, ExperimentEvent::TuningFinished(best.clone()));\n        Ok(best)\n    }\n}\n\npub struct RandomSearchTuner {\n    trials: usize,\n    seed: u64,\n}\nimpl RandomSearchTuner {\n    pub fn new(trials: usize, seed: u64) -> Self {\n        Self { trials, seed }\n    }\n}\nimpl<M: Trainable> TunerStrategy<M> for RandomSearchTuner {\n    fn tune(\n        &self,\n        space: HyperParamSpace<M>,\n        dataset: DatasetView,\n        observers: &[Arc<dyn ExperimentObserver<M>>],\n    ) -> Result<Option<TrialRecord<M>>, TuningError> {\n        let mut best: Option<TrialRecord<M>> = None;\n        let mut rng = StdRng::seed_from_u64(self.seed);\n\n        for _ in 0..self.trials {\n            let params = match space.pick_random(&mut rng) {\n                Some(p) => p,\n                None => return Err(TuningError::InvalidSpace(\"space is empty\".into())),\n            };\n\n            notify(observers, ExperimentEvent::TrialStarted(params.clone()));\n            let start = Instant::now();\n\n            let mut model = M::new(&params)?;\n            model.train(dataset.clone())?;\n            let metric = model.validate(dataset.clone())?;\n\n            let record = TrialRecord {\n                params: params.clone(),\n                metric,\n                duration_ms: start.elapsed().as_millis(),\n            };\n            notify(observers, ExperimentEvent::TrialCompleted(record.clone()));\n\n            if best.as_ref().map(|b| metric > b.metric).unwrap_or(true) {\n                best = Some(record);\n            }\n        }\n\n        notify(observers, ExperimentEvent::TuningFinished(best.clone()));\n        Ok(best)\n    }\n}\n\n// ------------------------------ Factory --------------------------------------\n#[derive(Clone, Copy)]\npub enum TunerKind {\n    Grid,\n    Random { trials: usize, seed: u64 },\n}\n\npub struct TunerFactory;\nimpl TunerFactory {\n    pub fn create<M: Trainable>(kind: TunerKind) -> Box<dyn TunerStrategy<M>> {\n        match kind {\n            TunerKind::Grid => Box::new(GridSearchTuner),\n            TunerKind::Random { trials, seed } => Box::new(RandomSearchTuner::new(trials, seed)),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ------------------------- Pipeline Orchestrator -----------------------------\npub async fn run_hyperparameter_tuning<M>(\n    kind: TunerKind,\n    space: HyperParamSpace<M>,\n    dataset: DatasetView,\n    observers: Vec<Arc<dyn ExperimentObserver<M>>>,\n) -> Result<Option<TrialRecord<M>>, TuningError>\nwhere\n    M: Trainable,\n{\n    let tuner = TunerFactory::create(kind);\n\n    // Offload to a blocking thread pool to avoid holding up async reactors.\n    let result = task::spawn_blocking(move || tuner.tune(space, dataset, &observers)).await?;\n\n    result\n}\n\n// ----------------------------- Helper Functions ------------------------------\nfn notify<M: Trainable>(observers: &[Arc<dyn ExperimentObserver<M>>], event: ExperimentEvent<M>) {\n    for obs in observers {\n        obs.on_event(event.clone());\n    }\n}\n\n// ------------------------ Mock Implementation (tests) -----------------------\n//! In real life, each domain model implements `Trainable` in its own crate.\n//! Below is a synthetic model so `cargo test` compiles without extra deps.\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\n\n    // Dummy model that tries to maximise x ‑ y.\n    #[derive(Debug)]\n    struct DummyModel {\n        x: i32,\n        y: i32,\n    }\n\n    #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n    struct DummyParams {\n        x: i32,\n        y: i32,\n    }\n\n    impl Trainable for DummyModel {\n        type HyperParams = DummyParams;\n        type Metric = i32;\n\n        fn new(params: &Self::HyperParams) -> Result<Self, TuningError> {\n            Ok(Self {\n                x: params.x,\n                y: params.y,\n            })\n        }\n\n        fn train(&mut self, _dataset: DatasetView) -> Result<(), TuningError> {\n            Ok(())\n        }\n\n        fn validate(&self, _dataset: DatasetView) -> Result<Self::Metric, TuningError> {\n            Ok(self.x - self.y)\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn sample_space() -> HyperParamSpace<DummyModel> {\n        let mut space = HyperParamSpace::<DummyModel>::new();\n        space.add_choice(\"x\", vec![1, 2, 3]);\n        space.add_choice(\"y\", vec![0, 1, 2]);\n        space\n    }\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_grid_search_finds_best() {\n        let dataset = DatasetView { id: \"dummy\".into() };\n        let obs = Arc::new(InMemoryObserver::<DummyModel>::default());\n\n        let best = run_hyperparameter_tuning(\n            TunerKind::Grid,\n            sample_space(),\n            dataset,\n            vec![obs.clone()],\n        )\n        .await\n        .unwrap()\n        .unwrap();\n\n        assert_eq!(best.metric, 3); {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01J2fKesZ7DpA5Z7dhHTHGZF","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→//! Generic utilities commonly used across VisuTility Orchestrator.\n     2→//!\n     3→//! The abstractions in this module are intentionally framework-agnostic:\n     4→//! they do not depend on any particular CV/ML crate and can therefore be\n     5→//! shared by every layer (Ingress, Data-Ops, Feature-Ops, …).\n     6→//!\n     7→//! All functions are `#[inline]` where it makes sense to do so, and\n     8→//! significant branches are annotated with `#[cold]` to let the optimizer\n     9→//! keep hot-paths small.\n    10→\n    11→use std::{\n    12→    fmt,\n    13→    fs::File,\n    14→    io::Read,\n    15→    path::{Path, PathBuf},\n    16→    sync::atomic::{AtomicUsize, Ordering},\n    17→    time::{Duration, Instant},\n    18→};\n    19→\n    20→use anyhow::{anyhow, Context, Result};\n    21→use once_cell::sync::Lazy;\n    22→use parking_lot::RwLock;\n    23→use serde::de::DeserializeOwned;\n    24→use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\n    25→use thiserror::Error;\n    26→use tracing::{debug, error, info, instrument};\n    27→\n    28→/// VisuTility's generic error type for utility-layer operations.\n    29→///\n    30→/// We prefer using [`anyhow::Error`] at the top-level application\n    31→/// boundary to allow for heterogeneous sources, but within the\n    32→/// utility layer we capture the most common failure categories so that\n    33→/// callers can still pattern-match when they need to.\n    34→#[derive(Debug, Error)]\n    35→pub enum UtilError {\n    36→    #[error(\"I/O error: {0}\")]\n    37→    Io(#[from] std::io::Error),\n    38→\n    39→    #[error(\"serialization error: {0}\")]\n    40→    Serde(#[from] serde_json::Error),\n    41→\n    42→    #[error(\"configuration not found at path `{0}`\")]\n    43→    ConfigNotFound(PathBuf),\n    44→\n    45→    #[error(\"task failed: {0}\")]\n    46→    TaskFailed(String),\n    47→}\n    48→\n    49→/// Generic wrapper around an [`Instant`] which logs the elapsed time at\n    50→/// **drop**.  The measurement is therefore *scoped*; as soon as the guard\n    51→/// goes out of scope, latency is emitted through the `tracing` facade.\n    52→///\n    53→/// ```no_run\n    54→/// use visu_tility_utils::ScopedTimer;\n    55→/// let _g = ScopedTimer::new(\"heavy_initialisation\");\n    56→/// // … some expensive code …\n    57→— guard drops here and we get a log line with\n    58→//   the elapsed time\n    59→/// ```\n    60→pub struct ScopedTimer<'a> {\n    61→    started: Instant,\n    62→    label: &'a str,\n    63→}\n    64→\n    65→impl<'a> ScopedTimer<'a> {\n    66→    /// Begin measuring elapsed time for the supplied label.\n    67→    #[inline]\n    68→    pub fn new(label: &'a str) -> Self {\n    69→        Self {\n    70→            started: Instant::now(),\n    71→            label,\n    72→        }\n    73→    }\n    74→\n    75→    /// Stop the timer and return the [`Duration`] since creation without\n    76→    /// logging. This allows manual control when automatic logging at `drop`\n    77→    /// is not desired.\n    78→    #[inline]\n    79→    pub fn stop(self) -> Duration {\n    80→        Instant::now() - self.started\n    81→    }\n    82→}\n    83→\n    84→impl<'a> Drop for ScopedTimer<'a> {\n    85→    fn drop(&mut self) {\n    86→        let elapsed = Instant::now() - self.started;\n    87→        info!(\n    88→            label = %self.label,\n    89→            ms = %elapsed.as_millis(),\n    90→            \"ScopedTimer finished\"\n    91→        );\n    92→        METRICS\n    93→            .write()\n    94→            .record_duration(self.label, elapsed.as_secs_f64());\n    95→    }\n    96→}\n    97→\n    98→/// Global metrics collector with *zero* external dependencies.  This is\n    99→/// intentionally minimal because higher layers might choose to forward these\n   100→/// numbers to Prometheus, StatsD, or another monitoring backend.\n   101→///\n   102→/// Internally it stores *exponential moving averages* so that it can be used\n   103→/// in hot-paths without unbounded memory growth.\n   104→pub struct Metrics {\n   105→    /// Moving average of durations (seconds) per label.\n   106→    durations: std::collections::HashMap<String, f64>,\n   107→    /// EWMA smoothing factor in `[0,1]`.\n   108→    alpha: f64,\n   109→}\n   110→\n   111→impl Metrics {\n   112→    fn new(alpha: f64) -> Self {\n   113→        Self {\n   114→            durations: std::collections::HashMap::new(),\n   115→            alpha,\n   116→        }\n   117→    }\n   118→\n   119→    /// Record a latency sample under `label`.\n   120→    pub fn record_duration(&mut self, label: impl Into<String>, secs: f64) {\n   121→        let label = label.into();\n   122→        let entry = self.durations.entry(label).or_insert(secs);\n   123→        // EWMA: v_t = alpha * x_t + (1-alpha) * v_{t-1}\n   124→        *entry = self.alpha * secs + (1.0 - self.alpha) * *entry;\n   125→    }\n   126→\n   127→    /// Return the current EWMA for `label`, if any.\n   128→    pub fn duration_avg(&self, label: &str) -> Option<f64> {\n   129→        self.durations.get(label).copied()\n   130→    }\n   131→}\n   132→\n   133→/// Static metrics instance; tweak the smoothing factor as needed.\n   134→/// We protect it with a *fair* RwLock to allow many concurrent readers.\n   135→static METRICS: Lazy<RwLock<Metrics>> = Lazy::new(|| RwLock::new(Metrics::new(0.20)));\n   136→\n   137→/// Typed representation of the global **application config**.\n   138→///\n   139→/// This is intentionally limited to fields that the utility layer needs\n   140→/// to know about; higher layers maintain their own extensions.\n   141→///\n   142→/// Because config files are versioned in the Model Registry, we include a\n   143→/// `schema_version` field to prevent silent breakage when the expected\n   144→/// structure changes.\n   145→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n   146→pub struct AppConfig {\n   147→    pub schema_version: u16,\n   148→    pub experiment_tracking_uri: Option<String>,\n   149→    pub model_registry_uri: Option<String>,\n   150→    pub default_retry: RetryConfig,\n   151→    /// Additional arbitrary key/value flavour that can be stored alongside\n   152→    /// the canonical config. Protects against accidental loading of huge blobs.\n   174→const MAX_CONFIG_SIZE: usize = 256 * 1024; // 256 KiB\n   175→\n   176→/// Load and parse application configuration **once** at start-up.\n   177→///\n   178→/// The file format is auto-detected based on extension (`.yaml`/`.yml` or\n   179→/// `.json`).  The resulting struct is cached in a global `Lazy`.\n   180→///\n   181→/// Example:\n   182→/// ```no_run\n   183→/// let cfg = visu_tility_utils::config();\n   184→/// println!(\"Schema v{} loaded\", cfg.schema_version);\n   185→/// ```\n   186→fn read_config_from_disk(path: &Path) -> Result<AppConfig> {\n   187→    if !path.exists() {\n   188→        return Err(UtilError::ConfigNotFound(path.into()).into());\n   189→    }\n   190→\n   191→    let mut file =\n   192→        File::open(path).with_context(|| format!(\"opening configuration file `{}`\", path.display()))?;\n   193→\n   194→    let mut buf = Vec::new();\n   195→    file.read_to_end(&mut buf)\n   196→        .with_context(|| format!(\"reading configuration file `{}`\", path.display()))?;\n   197→\n   198→    if buf.len() > MAX_CONFIG_SIZE {\n   199→        return Err(anyhow!(\n   200→            \"configuration file `{}` larger than {} KiB\",\n   201→            path.display(),\n   202→            MAX_CONFIG_SIZE / 1024\n   203→        ));\n   204→    }\n   205→\n   206→    let cfg = match path.extension().and_then(|s| s.to_str()) {\n   207→        Some(\"yaml\") | Some(\"yml\") => serde_yaml::from_slice(&buf)?,\n   208→        Some(\"json\") => serde_json::from_slice(&buf)?,\n   209→        other => {\n   210→            return Err(anyhow!(\n   211→                \"unsupported configuration extension {:?} for `{}`\",\n   212→                other,\n   213→                path.display()\n   214→            ))\n   215→        }\n   216→    };\n   217→\n   218→    Ok(cfg)\n   219→}\n   220→\n   221→/// Discover the config path in the following order:\n   222→///\n   223→/// 1. 227→    if let Ok(path) = std::env::var(\"VISU_CONFIG\") {\n   228→        return Some(PathBuf::from(path));\n   229→    }\n   230→\n   231→    const CANDIDATES: &[&str] = &[\"visu_config.yaml\",    }\n   237→    }\n   238→    None\n   239→}\n   240→\n   241→/// Global application configuration singleton.\n   242→static CONFIG: Lazy<AppConfig> = Lazy::new(|| {\n   243→    let path = ```\n   294→pub fn retry<F, T, E>(cfg: &RetryConfig, mut f: F) -> Result<T, E>\n   295→where\n   296→    F: FnMut() -> Result<T, E>,\n   297→{\n   298→    let mut attempt = 0;\n   299→    loop {\n   300→        match f() {\n   301→            Ok(v) => return Ok(v),\n   302→            Err(e) if attempt + 1 == cfg.max_attempts => return Err(e),\n   303→            Err(_e) => {\n   304→                attempt += 1;\n   305→                std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(cfg.backoff_millis));\n   306→            }\n   307→        }\n   308→    }\n   309→}\n   310→\n   311→/// Simple **ID 340→    }\n   341→}\n   342→\n   343→/// **Observer pattern**: runtime event bus.\n   344→//\n   345→/// Very light-weight publisher/subscriber abstraction built on top of\n   346→/// errors are logged but otherwise ignored since the\n   381→        /// system should remain functional even when no-one is listening.\n   382→        pub fn publish(&self, evt: Event) {\n   383→            if let Err(e) = self.tx.send(evt.clone()) {\n   384→                debug!(error = ?e, event = ?evt, \"failed to publish event\");\n   385→            }\n   386→        }\n   387→    }\n   388→\n   389→    /// A stream of events.\n   390→    pub type Subscription = Receiver<Event>;\n   391→\n   392→    /// Construct a **single** global bus so that layers can obtain\n   393→    /// cheap clones of the `Publisher` without having to pass them around.\n   394→    static BUS: Lazy<(Publisher, Subscription)> = Lazy::new(|| {\n   395→        let (tx, rx) = unbounded::<Event>();\n   396→        (Publisher { tx }, rx)\n   397→    });\n   398→\n   399→    /// Return a handle for *publishing* events.\n   400→    pub fn publisher() -> Publisher {\n   401→        BUS.0.clone()\n   402→    }\n   403→\n   404→    /// Subscribe to events.  Every caller receives a *clone* of the receiver\n   405→    /// which means all subscribers see *all* events (fan-out).\n   406→    pub fn subscribe() -> Subscription {\n   407→        BUS.1.clone()\n   408→    }\n   409→}\n   410→\n   411→/// A small proc-macro-like helper (implemented as declarative macro to avoid meta\n   412→/// crate) for scoped timing.\n   413→///\n   414→/// ```\n   415→/// visu_tility_utils::scope_time!(\"fancy_stage\", {\n   416→///     expensive();\n   417→/// });\n   418→/// ```\n   419→#[macro_export]\n   420→macro_rules! scope_time {\n   421→    ($label:expr, $block:block) => {{\n   422→        let _g = $crate::ScopedTimer::new($label);\n   423→        $block\n   424→    }};\n   425→}\n   426→\n   427→/// Tests live inside the same file so that the utils crate remains\n   428→/// a **single** compilation unit. Generic utilities commonly used across VisuTility Orchestrator.\n//!\n//! The abstractions in this module are intentionally framework-agnostic:\n//! they do not depend on any particular CV/ML crate and can therefore be\n//! shared by every layer (Ingress, Data-Ops, Feature-Ops, …).\n//!\n//! All functions are `#[inline]` where it makes sense to do so, and\n//! significant branches are annotated with `#[cold]` to let the optimizer\n//! keep hot-paths small.\n\nuse std::{\n    fmt,\n    fs::File,\n    io::Read,\n    path::{Path, PathBuf},\n    sync::atomic::{AtomicUsize, Ordering},\n    time::{Duration, Instant},\n};\n\nuse anyhow::{anyhow, Context, Result};\nuse once_cell::sync::Lazy;\nuse parking_lot::RwLock;\nuse serde::de::DeserializeOwned;\nuse serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\nuse thiserror::Error;\nuse tracing::{debug, error, info, instrument};\n\n/// VisuTility's generic error type for utility-layer operations.\n///\n/// We prefer using [`anyhow::Error`] at the top-level application\n/// boundary to allow for heterogeneous sources, but within the\n/// utility layer we capture the most common failure categories so that\n/// callers can still pattern-match when they need to.\n#[derive(Debug, Error)]\npub enum UtilError {\n    #[error(\"I/O error: {0}\")]\n    Io(#[from] std::io::Error),\n\n    #[error(\"serialization error: {0}\")]\n    Serde(#[from] serde_json::Error),\n\n    #[error(\"configuration not found at path `{0}`\")]\n    ConfigNotFound(PathBuf),\n\n    #[error(\"task failed: {0}\")]\n    TaskFailed(String),\n}\n\n/// Generic wrapper around an [`Instant`] which logs the elapsed time at\n/// **drop**.  The measurement is therefore *scoped*; as soon as the guard\n/// goes out of scope, latency is emitted through the `tracing` facade.\n///\n/// ```no_run\n/// use visu_tility_utils::ScopedTimer;\n/// let _g = ScopedTimer::new(\"heavy_initialisation\");\n/// // … some expensive code …\n— guard drops here and we get a log line with\n//   the elapsed time\n/// ```\npub struct ScopedTimer<'a> {\n    started: Instant,\n    label: &'a str,\n}\n\nimpl<'a> ScopedTimer<'a> {\n    /// Begin measuring elapsed time for the supplied label.\n    #[inline]\n    pub fn new(label: &'a str) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            started: Instant::now(),\n            label,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Stop the timer and return the [`Duration`] since creation without\n    /// logging. This allows manual control when automatic logging at `drop`\n    /// is not desired.\n    #[inline]\n    pub fn stop(self) -> Duration {\n        Instant::now() - self.started\n    }\n}\n\nimpl<'a> Drop for ScopedTimer<'a> {\n    fn drop(&mut self) {\n        let elapsed = Instant::now() - self.started;\n        info!(\n            label = %self.label,\n            ms = %elapsed.as_millis(),\n            \"ScopedTimer finished\"\n        );\n        METRICS\n            .write()\n            .record_duration(self.label, elapsed.as_secs_f64());\n    }\n}\n\n/// Global metrics collector with *zero* external dependencies. The resulting struct is cached in a global `Lazy`.\n///\n/// Example:\n/// ```no_run\n/// let cfg = visu_tility_utils::config();\n/// println!(\"Schema v{} loaded\", cfg.schema_version);\n/// ```\nfn read_config_from_disk(path: &Path) -> Result<AppConfig> {\n    if !path.exists() {\n        return Err(UtilError::ConfigNotFound(path.into()).into());\n    }\n\n    let mut file =\n        File::open(path).with_context(|| format!(\"opening configuration file `{}`\", path.display()))?;\n\n    let mut buf = Vec::new();\n    file.read_to_end(&mut buf)\n        .with_context(|| format!(\"reading configuration file `{}`\", path.display()))?;\n\n    if buf.len() > MAX_CONFIG_SIZE {\n        return Err(anyhow!(\n            \"configuration file `{}` larger than {} KiB\",\n            path.display(),\n            MAX_CONFIG_SIZE / 1024\n        ));\n    }\n\n    let cfg = match path.extension().and_then(|s| s.to_str()) {\n        Some(\"yaml\") | Some(\"yml\") => serde_yaml::from_slice(&buf)?,\n        Some(\"json\") => serde_json::from_slice(&buf)?,\n        other => {\n            return Err(anyhow!(\n                \"unsupported configuration extension {:?} for `{}`\",\n                other,\n                path.display()\n            ))\n        }\n    };\n\n    Ok(cfg)\n}\n\n/// Discover the config path in the following order:\n///\n/// 1. until it either succeeds or\n/// max_attempts have been reached.\n///\n/// Usage:\n/// ```ignore\n/// use visu_tility_utils::retry;\n///\n/// let result = retry(\n///     &config().default_retry,\n///     || {\n///         // call external dependency that might fail\n///         some_unstable_operation()\n///     });\n/// ```\npub fn retry<F, T, E>(cfg: &RetryConfig, mut f: F) -> Result<T, E>\nwhere\n    F: FnMut() -> Result<T, E>,\n{\n    let mut attempt = 0;\n    loop {\n        match f() {\n            Ok(v) => return Ok(v),\n            Err(e) if attempt + 1 == cfg.max_attempts => return Err(e),\n            Err(_e) => {\n                attempt += 1;\n                std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(cfg.backoff_millis));\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// {\n    /// Allocate a fresh id.\n    #[inline]\n    pub fn fresh() -> Self {\n        let n = COUNTER.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);\n        Id(n as u64)\n    }\n\n    /// Return the underlying integer.\n    #[inline]\n    pub fn as_u64(self) -> u64 {\n        self.0\n    }\n}\n\nimpl fmt::Display for Id {\n    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {\n        self.0.fmt(f)\n    }\n}\n\n/// **Observer pattern**: runtime event bus.\n//\n/// Very light-weight publisher/subscriber abstraction built on top of\n/// [`crossbeam_channel`].  Since layers of the Orchestrator run in\n/// different scheduling contexts we cannot rely on *any* runtime; a\n/// channel-based design has zero dependencies on async executors.\npub mod event_bus {\n    use super::*;\n    use crossbeam_channel::{unbounded, Receiver, Sender};\n\n    /// Event types that travel on the bus.  This enum grows over time as new\n    /// features make use of the bus; by keeping it located in a *utility*\n    /// crate we avoid circular dependencies.\n    #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n    pub enum Event {\n        HyperparameterTuningStarted { experiment_id: Id },\n        HyperparameterTuningFinished { experiment_id: Id },\n        ModelRegistered {\n            name: String,\n            version: String,\n            metadata: std::collections::HashMap<String, String>,\n        },\n        ModelDriftDetected {\n            model: String,\n            magnitude: f32,\n            timestamp: chrono::DateTime<chrono::Utc>,\n        },\n        Custom(String, serde_json::Value),\n    }\n\n    /// Handle required for publishing events.\n    #[derive(Clone)]\n    pub struct Publisher {\n        tx: Sender<Event>,\n    }\n\n    impl Publisher {\n        /// Publish an event; errors are logged but otherwise ignored since the\n        /// system should remain functional even when no-one is listening.\n        pub fn publish(&self, evt: Event) {\n            if let Err(e) = self.tx.send(evt.clone()) {\n                debug!(error = ?e, event = ?evt, \"failed to publish event\");\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// A stream of events.\n    pub type Subscription = Receiver<Event>;\n\n    /// Construct a **single** global bus so that layers can obtain\n    /// cheap clones of the `Publisher` without having to pass them around.\n    static BUS: Lazy<(Publisher, Subscription)> = Lazy::new(|| {\n        let (tx, rx) = unbounded::<Event>();\n        (Publisher { tx }, rx)\n    });\n\n    /// Return a handle for *publishing* events.\n    pub fn publisher() -> Publisher {\n        BUS.0.clone()\n    }\n\n    /// Subscribe to events.  Every caller receives a *clone* of the receiver\n    /// which means all subscribers see *all* events (fan-out).\n    pub fn subscribe() -> Subscription {\n        BUS.1.clone()\n    }\n}\n\n/// A small proc-macro-like helper (implemented as declarative macro to avoid meta\n/// crate) for scoped timing.\n///\n/// ```\n/// visu_tility_utils::scope_time!(\"fancy_stage\", {\n///     expensive();\n/// });\n/// ```\n#[macro_export]\nmacro_rules! scope_time {\n    ($label:expr, $block:block) => {{\n        let _g = $crate::ScopedTimer::new($label);\n        $block\n    }};\n}\n\n/// Tests live inside the same file so that the utils crate remains\n/// a **single** compilation unit.  This is important for global singletons\n/// (`Lazy`) which must not be duplicated across multiple crates.\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n\n    #[test]\n    fn id_monotonicity() {\n        let a = Id::fresh();\n        let b = Id::fresh();\n        assert!(b.as_u64() > a.as_u64());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn retry_succeeds_eventually() {\n        let cfg = RetryConfig {\n            max_attempts: 5,\n            backoff_millis: 1,\n        };\n        let mut n = 0;\n        let res: Result<usize, usize> = retry(&cfg, || {\n            if n < 3 {\n                n += 1;\n                Err(n)\n            } else {\n                Ok(n)\n            }\n        });\n        assert_eq!(res.unwrap(), 3);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn metrics_ewma() {\n        METRICS.write().record_duration(\"foo\", 1.0);\n        METRICS.write().record_duration(\"foo\", 1.0);\n        let avg = METRICS.read().duration_avg(\"foo\").unwrap();\n        assert!(avg > 0.0 && avg <= 1.0);\n    }\n}","numLines":466,"startLine":1,"totalLines":466}}}
