{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_019TeLj3xcvwbZYTHeffoCcV","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→```rust\n     2→//! Hyper-parameter Tuning Engine\n     3→//!\n     4→//! This module implements a Strategy-Pattern–based hyper-parameter tuning\n     5→//! engine that can be plugged into any stage of the VisuTility Orchestrator\n     6→//! pipeline. The engine provides:\n     7→//!   • A generic [`Tuner`] façade that coordinates trial execution\n     8→//!   • Pluggable tuning strategies (e.g. Grid, Random, Bayesian)\n     9→//!   • Observer hooks for experiment tracking & early-stopping\n    10→//!\n    11→//! The code purposefully avoids project-specific dependencies so it can be\n    12→//! re-used by other crates within the workspace. All heavy lifting (model\n    13→//! compilation, dataset loading, etc.) is delegated to an [`Objective`]\n    14→//! implementation supplied by the caller.\n    15→\n    16→use std::collections::HashMap;\n    17→use std::fmt;\n    18→use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};\n    19→use std::time::{Duration, Instant};\n    20→\n    21→use crossbeam_channel::{unbounded, Receiver, Sender};\n    22→use rand::distributions::{Distribution, Uniform};\n    23→use rand::{rngs::StdRng, SeedableRng};\n    24→use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\n    25→use thiserror::Error;\n    26→\n    27→/// Generic error type for the tuning engine.\n    28→#[derive(Debug, Error)]\n    29→pub enum TuningError {\n    30→    #[error(\"objective function failed: {0}\")]\n    31→    ObjectiveFailed(String),\n    32→\n    33→    #[error(\"invalid parameter space definition: {0}\")]\n    34→    InvalidParameter(String),\n    35→\n    36→    #[error(\"I/O error: {0}\")]\n    37→    Io(#[from] std::io::Error),\n    38→\n    39→    #[error(\"serialization error: {0}\")]\n    40→    Serde(#[from] serde_json::Error),\n    41→}\n    42→\n    43→/// Domain of a single hyper-parameter.\n    44→///\n    45→/// The enum is intentionally limited to float & discrete types to keep the\n    46→/// sample code concise. In production you may want Boolean, Categorical, etc.\n    47→#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n    48→pub enum ParameterDomain {\n    49→    Continuous { low: f64, high: f64 },\n    50→    Discrete(Vec<f64>),\n    51→}\n    52→\n    53→impl ParameterDomain {\n    54→    /// Sample a value from the domain.\n    55→    fn sample<R: rand::Rng>(&self, rng: &mut R) -> f64 {\n    56→        match self {\n    57→            ParameterDomain::Continuous { low, high } => {\n    58→                let distr = Uniform::new(*low, *high);\n    59→                distr.sample(rng)\n    60→            }\n    61→            ParameterDomain::Discrete(values) => {\n    62→                let idx = rng.gen_range(0..values.len());\n    63→                values[idx]\n    64→            }\n    65→        }\n    66→    }\n    67→\n    68→    /// Iterate over all discrete points when applicable.\n    69→    fn iter(&self) -> Option<impl Iterator<Item = f64> + '_> {\n    70→        match self {\n    71→            ParameterDomain::Discrete(values) => Some(values.iter().copied()),\n    72→            _ => None,\n    73→        }\n    74→    }\n    75→}\n    76→\n    77→/// Collection of named domains.\n    78→pub type ParameterSpace = HashMap<String, ParameterDomain>;\n    79→\n    80→/// Concrete set of hyper-parameters for a single trial.\n    81→pub type HyperParams = HashMap<String, f64>;\n    82→\n    83→/// Result produced by a single trial.\n    84→#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n    85→pub struct TrialResult {\n    86→    pub params: HyperParams,\n    87→    pub score: f64,\n    88→    pub duration: Duration,\n    89→}\n    90→\n    91→/// Contract for objective evaluation.\n    92→///\n    93→/// Implementors should train a model (or a down-scaled proxy) and return the\n    94→/// metric that the tuning process must optimize (higher is better).\n    95→pub trait Objective: Send + Sync + 'static {\n    96→    fn evaluate(&self, params: &HyperParams) -> Result<f64, TuningError>;\n    97→}\n    98→\n    99→/// Observer pattern for side-effects such as logging, experiment tracking,\n   100→/// early stopping, etc.\n   101→pub trait TuningObserver: Send + Sync {\n   102→    fn on_trial_completed(&self, result: &TrialResult);\n   103→}\n   104→\n   105→/// Strategy pattern for search algorithms.\n   106→pub trait TuningStrategy: Send + Sync {\n   107→    fn next_parameters(&mut self) -> Option<HyperParams>;\n   108→    fn register_result(&mut self, _result: &TrialResult) {}\n   109→}\n   110→\n   111→/// Grid Search implementation.\n   112→///\n   113→/// Explores the cartesian product of every discrete domain, *ignoring*\n   114→/// continuous ones. If the user provides only continuous spaces, the grid is\n   115→/// empty and `next_parameters` will always return `None`.\n   116→pub struct GridSearch {\n   117→    grid: Vec<HyperParams>,\n   118→    cursor: usize,\n   119→}\n   120→\n   121→impl GridSearch {\n   122→    pub fn new(space: &ParameterSpace) -> Self {\n   123→        let mut grid: Vec<HyperParams> = vec![HyperParams::new()];\n   124→        for (name, domain) in space {\n   125→            if let Some(iter) = domain.iter() {\n   126→                let mut next_grid = Vec::with_capacity(grid.len() * 4);\n   127→                for existing in &grid {\n   128→                    for value in iter.clone() {\n   129→                        let mut params = existing.clone();\n   130→                        params.insert(name.clone(), value);\n   131→                        next_grid.push(params);\n   132→                    }\n   133→                }\n   134→                grid = next_grid;\n   135→            }\n   136→        }\n   137→        Self { grid, cursor: 0 }\n   138→    }\n   139→}\n   140→\n   141→impl TuningStrategy for GridSearch {\n   142→    fn next_parameters(&mut self) -> Option<HyperParams> {\n   143→        if self.cursor < self.grid.len() {\n   144→            let params = self.grid[self.cursor].clone();\n   145→            self.cursor += 1;\n   146→            Some(params)\n   147→        } else {\n   148→            None\n   149→        }\n   150→    }\n   151→}\n   152→\n   153→/// Random Search implementation using a seeded PRNG.\n   154→pub struct RandomSearch {\n   155→    space: ParameterSpace,\n   156→    rng: StdRng,\n   157→    max_trials: usize,\n   158→    generated: usize,\n   159→}\n   160→\n   161→impl RandomSearch {\n   162→    pub fn new(space: ParameterSpace, max_trials: usize, seed: u64) -> Self {\n   163→        Self {\n   164→            space,\n   165→            rng: StdRng::seed_from_u64(seed),\n   166→            max_trials,\n   167→            generated: 0,\n   168→        }\n   169→    }\n   170→}\n   171→\n   172→impl TuningStrategy for RandomSearch {\n   173→    fn next_parameters(&mut self) -> Option<HyperParams> {\n   174→        if self.generated >= self.max_trials {\n   175→            return None;\n   176→        }\n   177→        let mut params = HyperParams::with_capacity(self.space.len());\n   178→        for (name, domain) in &self.space {\n   179→            params.insert(name.clone(), domain.sample(&mut self.rng));\n   180→        }\n   181→        self.generated += 1;\n   182→        Some(params)\n   183→    }\n   184→}\n   185→\n   186→/// Thread-safe experiment tracker that writes each trial to a JSON Lines file.\n   187→/// The implementation is intentionally simple and can be replaced by something\n   188→/// like MLflow, Weights & Biases, or a SQL database.\n   189→pub struct JsonlTracker {\n   190→    file: Arc<Mutex<std::fs::File>>,\n   191→}\n   192→\n   193→impl JsonlTracker {\n   194→    pub fn new(path: impl AsRef<std::path::Path>) -> Result<Self, TuningError> {\n   195→        let file = std::fs::OpenOptions::new()\n   196→            .create(true)\n   197→            .append(true)\n   198→            .open(path)?;\n   199→        Ok(Self {\n   200→            file: Arc::new(Mutex::new(file)),\n   201→        })\n   202→    }\n   203→}\n   204→\n   205→impl TuningObserver for JsonlTracker {\n   206→    fn on_trial_completed(&self, result: &TrialResult) {\n   207→        if let Ok(serialized) = serde_json::to_string(result) {\n   208→            if let Ok(mut file) = self.file.lock() {\n   209→                // Ignore I/O errors so tuning can proceed.\n   210→                let _ = writeln!(file, \"{}\", serialized);\n   211→            }\n   212→        }\n   213→    }\n   214→}\n   215→\n   216→/// Central façade that orchestrates strategy, observers and objective.\n   217→pub struct Tuner<S: TuningStrategy> {\n   218→    strategy: S,\n   219→    objective: Arc<dyn Objective>,\n   220→    observers: Vec<Arc<dyn TuningObserver>>,\n   221→    tx: Sender<TrialResult>,\n   222→    rx: Receiver<TrialResult>,\n   223→}\n   224→\n   225→impl<S: TuningStrategy> Tuner<S> {\n   226→    pub fn builder(strategy: S, objective: Arc<dyn Objective>) -> TunerBuilder<S> {\n   227→        TunerBuilder::new(strategy, objective)\n   228→    }\n   229→\n   230→    fn new(strategy: S, objective: Arc<dyn Objective>, observers: Vec<Arc<dyn TuningObserver>>) -> Self {\n   231→        let (tx, rx) = unbounded();\n   232→        Self {\n   233→            strategy,\n   234→            objective,\n   235→            observers,\n   236→            tx,\n   237→            rx,\n   238→        }\n   239→    }\n   240→\n   241→    /// Drives the tuning loop until the strategy is exhausted or an error\n   242→    /// occurs. Returns the best trial encountered.\n   243→    pub fn run(&mut self) -> Result<TrialResult, TuningError> {\n   244→        let mut best: Option<TrialResult> = None;\n   245→\n   246→        // Spawn a simple worker thread pool (1 thread / logical core).\n   247→        let n_workers = num_cpus::get();\n   248→        let mut handles = Vec::with_capacity(n_workers);\n   249→        for _ in 0..n_workers {\n   250→            let tx = self.tx.clone();\n   251→            let objective = self.objective.clone();\n   252→            let mut local_strategy = &mut self.strategy as *mut S; // raw ptr to share across threads\n   253→            handles.push(std::thread::spawn(move || loop {\n   254→                // SAFETY: Exclusive access is guaranteed by the global lock below.\n   255→                let params = {\n   256→                    // Strategy access is synchronized via a global mutex.\n   257→                    static STRATEGY_LOCK: once_cell::sync::Lazy<Mutex<()>> =\n   258→                        once_cell::sync::Lazy::new(|| Mutex::new(()));\n   259→                    let _guard = STRATEGY_LOCK.lock().unwrap();\n   260→                    // Mutable borrow of the strategy behind the raw pointer.\n   261→                    unsafe { &mut *local_strategy }.next_parameters()\n   262→                };\n   263→\n   264→                let params = match params {\n   265→                    Some(p) => p,\n   266→                    None => break, // no more work\n   267→                };\n   268→\n   269→                let time_start = Instant::now();\n   270→                let score = match objective.evaluate(&params) {\n   271→                    Ok(s) => s,\n   272→                    Err(e) => {\n   273→                        let _ = tx.send(TrialResult {\n   274→                            params,\n   275→                            score: f64::NAN,\n   276→                            duration: time_start.elapsed(),\n   277→                        });\n   278→                        eprintln!(\"Objective failed: {e}\");\n   279→                        continue;\n   280→                    }\n   281→                };\n   282→\n   283→                let result = TrialResult {\n   284→                    params,\n   285→                    score,\n   286→                    duration: time_start.elapsed(),\n   287→                };\n   288→                let _ = tx.send(result);\n   289→            }));\n   290→        }\n   291→\n   292→        drop(self.tx); This allows the tuner to finish quickly.\n   344→#[cfg(test)]\n   345→mod tests {\n   346→    use super::*;\n   347→\n   348→    struct DummyObjective;\n   349→\n   350→    impl Objective for DummyObjective {\n   351→        fn evaluate(&self, params: &HyperParams) -> Result<f64, TuningError> {\n   352→            let x = params.get(\"x\").copied().unwrap_or(0.0);\n   353→            let y = params.get(\"y\").copied().unwrap_or(0.0);\n   354→            // simple 2-D inverted paraboloid + random noise\n   355→            let value = -(x - Hyper-parameter Tuning Engine\n//!\n//! This module implements a Strategy-Pattern–based hyper-parameter tuning\n//! engine that can be plugged into any stage of the VisuTility Orchestrator\n//! pipeline. The engine provides:\n//!   • A generic [`Tuner`] façade that coordinates trial execution\n//!   • Pluggable tuning strategies (e.g. Grid, Random, Bayesian)\n//!   • Observer hooks for experiment tracking & early-stopping\n//!\n//! The code purposefully avoids project-specific dependencies so it can be\n//! re-used by other crates within the workspace. All heavy lifting (model\n//! compilation, dataset loading, etc.) is delegated to an [`Objective`]\n//! implementation supplied by the caller.\n\nuse std::collections::HashMap;\nuse std::fmt;\nuse std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};\nuse std::time::{Duration, Instant};\n\nuse crossbeam_channel::{unbounded, Receiver, Sender};\nuse rand::distributions::{Distribution, Uniform};\nuse rand::{rngs::StdRng, SeedableRng};\nuse serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\nuse thiserror::Error;\n\n/// Generic error type for the tuning engine.\n#[derive(Debug, Error)]\npub enum TuningError {\n    #[error(\"objective function failed: {0}\")]\n    ObjectiveFailed(String),\n\n    #[error(\"invalid parameter space definition: {0}\")]\n    InvalidParameter(String),\n\n    #[error(\"I/O error: {0}\")]\n    Io(#[from] std::io::Error),\n\n    #[error(\"serialization error: {0}\")]\n    Serde(#[from] serde_json::Error),\n}\n\n/// Domain of a single hyper-parameter.\n///\n/// The enum is intentionally limited to float & discrete types to keep the\n/// sample code concise. In production you may want Boolean, Categorical, etc.\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub enum ParameterDomain {\n    Continuous { low: f64, high: f64 },\n    Discrete(Vec<f64>),\n}\n\nimpl ParameterDomain {\n    /// Sample a value from the domain.\n    fn sample<R: rand::Rng>(&self, rng: &mut R) -> f64 {\n        match self {\n            ParameterDomain::Continuous { low, high } => {\n                let distr = Uniform::new(*low, *high);\n                distr.sample(rng)\n            }\n            ParameterDomain::Discrete(values) => {\n                let idx = rng.gen_range(0..values.len());\n                values[idx]\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Iterate over all discrete points when applicable.\n    fn iter(&self) -> Option<impl Iterator<Item = f64> + '_> {\n        match self {\n            ParameterDomain::Discrete(values) => Some(values.iter().copied()),\n            _ => None,\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Collection of named domains.\npub type ParameterSpace = HashMap<String, ParameterDomain>;\n\n/// Concrete set of hyper-parameters for a single trial.\npub type HyperParams = HashMap<String, f64>;\n\n/// Result produced by a single trial.\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct TrialResult {\n    pub params: HyperParams,\n    pub score: f64,\n    pub duration: Duration,\n}\n\n/// Contract for objective evaluation.\n///\n/// Implementors should train a model (or a down-scaled proxy) and return the\n/// metric that the tuning process must optimize (higher is better).\npub trait Objective: Send + Sync + 'static {\n    fn evaluate(&self, params: &HyperParams) -> Result<f64, TuningError>;\n}\n\n/// Observer pattern for side-effects such as logging, experiment tracking,\n/// early stopping, etc.\npub trait TuningObserver: Send + Sync {\n    fn on_trial_completed(&self, result: &TrialResult);\n}\n\n/// Strategy pattern for search algorithms.\npub trait TuningStrategy: Send + Sync {\n    fn next_parameters(&mut self) -> Option<HyperParams>;\n    fn register_result(&mut self, _result: &TrialResult) {}\n}\n\n/// Grid Search implementation.\n///\n/// Explores the cartesian product of every discrete domain, *ignoring*\n/// continuous ones. If the user provides only continuous spaces, the grid is\n/// empty and `next_parameters` will always return `None`.\npub struct GridSearch {\n    grid: Vec<HyperParams>,\n    cursor: usize,\n}\n\nimpl GridSearch {\n    pub fn new(space: &ParameterSpace) -> Self {\n        let mut grid: Vec<HyperParams> = vec![HyperParams::new()];\n        for (name, domain) in space {\n            if let Some(iter) = domain.iter() {\n                let mut next_grid = Vec::with_capacity(grid.len() * 4);\n                for existing in &grid {\n                    for value in iter.clone() {\n                        let mut params = existing.clone();\n                        params.insert(name.clone(), value);\n                        next_grid.push(params);\n                    }\n                }\n                grid = next_grid;\n            }\n        }\n        Self { grid, cursor: 0 }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl TuningStrategy for GridSearch {\n    fn next_parameters(&mut self) -> Option<HyperParams> {\n        if self.cursor < self.grid.len() {\n            let params = self.grid[self.cursor].clone();\n            self.cursor += 1;\n            Some(params)\n        } else {\n            None\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Random Search implementation using a seeded PRNG.\npub struct RandomSearch {\n    space: ParameterSpace,\n    rng: StdRng,\n    max_trials: usize,\n    generated: usize,\n}\n\nimpl RandomSearch {\n    pub fn new(space: ParameterSpace, max_trials: usize, seed: u64) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            space,\n            rng: StdRng::seed_from_u64(seed),\n            max_trials,\n            generated: 0,\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl TuningStrategy for RandomSearch {\n    fn next_parameters(&mut self) -> Option<HyperParams> {\n        if self.generated >= self.max_trials {\n            return None;\n        }\n        let mut params = HyperParams::with_capacity(self.space.len());\n        for (name, domain) in &self.space {\n            params.insert(name.clone(), domain.sample(&mut self.rng));\n        }\n        self.generated += 1;\n        Some(params)\n    }\n}\n\n/// Thread-safe experiment tracker that writes each trial to a JSON Lines file.\n/// The implementation is intentionally simple and can be replaced by something\n/// like MLflow, Weights & Biases, or a SQL database.\npub struct JsonlTracker {\n    file: Arc<Mutex<std::fs::File>>,\n}\n\nimpl JsonlTracker {\n    pub fn new(path: impl AsRef<std::path::Path>) -> Result<Self, TuningError> {\n        let file = std::fs::OpenOptions::new()\n            .create(true)\n            .append(true)\n            .open(path)?;\n        Ok(Self {\n            file: Arc::new(Mutex::new(file)),\n        })\n    }\n}\n\nimpl TuningObserver for JsonlTracker {\n    fn on_trial_completed(&self, result: &TrialResult) {\n        if let Ok(serialized) = serde_json::to_string(result) {\n            if let Ok(mut file) = self.file.lock() {\n                // Ignore I/O errors so tuning can proceed.\n                let _ = writeln!(file, \"{}\", serialized);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Central façade that orchestrates strategy, observers and objective.\npub struct Tuner<S: TuningStrategy> {\n    strategy: S,\n    objective: Arc<dyn Objective>,\n    observers: Vec<Arc<dyn TuningObserver>>,\n    tx: Sender<TrialResult>,\n    rx: Receiver<TrialResult>,\n}\n\nimpl<S: TuningStrategy> Tuner<S> {\n    pub fn builder(strategy: S, objective: Arc<dyn Objective>) -> TunerBuilder<S> {\n        TunerBuilder::new(strategy, objective)\n    }\n\n    fn new(strategy: S, objective: Arc<dyn Objective>, observers: Vec<Arc<dyn TuningObserver>>) -> Self {\n        let (tx, rx) = unbounded();\n        Self {\n            strategy,\n            objective,\n            observers,\n            tx,\n            rx,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Drives the tuning loop until the strategy is exhausted or an error\n    /// occurs. Returns the best trial encountered.\n    pub fn run(&mut self) -> Result<TrialResult, TuningError> {\n        let mut best: Option<TrialResult> = None;\n\n        // Spawn a simple worker thread pool (1 thread / logical core).\n        let n_workers = num_cpus::get();\n        let mut handles = Vec::with_capacity(n_workers);\n        for _ in 0..n_workers {\n            let tx = self.tx.clone();\n            let objective = self.objective.clone();\n            let mut local_strategy = &mut self.strategy as *mut S; // raw ptr to share across threads\n            handles.push(std::thread::spawn(move || loop {\n                // SAFETY: Exclusive access is guaranteed by the global lock below.\n                let params = {\n                    // Strategy access is synchronized via a global mutex.\n                    static STRATEGY_LOCK: once_cell::sync::Lazy<Mutex<()>> =\n                        once_cell::sync::Lazy::new(|| Mutex::new(()));\n                    let _guard = STRATEGY_LOCK.lock().unwrap();\n                    // Mutable borrow of the strategy behind the raw pointer.\n                    unsafe { &mut *local_strategy }.next_parameters()\n                };\n\n                let params = match params {\n                    Some(p) => p,\n                    None => break, // no more work\n                };\n\n                let time_start = Instant::now();\n                let score = match objective.evaluate(&params) {\n                    Ok(s) => s,\n                    Err(e) => {\n                        let _ = tx.send(TrialResult {\n                            params,\n                            score: f64::NAN,\n                            duration: time_start.elapsed(),\n                        });\n                        eprintln!(\"Objective failed: {e}\");\n                        continue;\n                    }\n                };\n\n                let result = TrialResult {\n                    params,\n                    score,\n                    duration: time_start.elapsed(),\n                };\n                let _ = tx.send(result);\n            }));\n        }\n\n        drop(self.tx); // Close senders when workers finish.\n\n        // Collect results & notify observers.\n        for trial in &self.rx {\n            self.strategy.register_result(&trial);\n            for obs in &self.observers {\n                obs.on_trial_completed(&trial);\n            }\n            if best.as_ref().map_or(true, |b| trial.score > b.score) {\n                best = Some(trial);\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Wait for threads to finish.\n        for h in handles {\n            let _ = h.join();\n        }\n\n        best.ok_or_else(|| TuningError::InvalidParameter(\"No trials executed\".into()))\n    }\n}\n\n/// Builder for [`Tuner`] allowing ergonomic configuration.\npub struct TunerBuilder<S: TuningStrategy> {\n    strategy: S,\n    objective: Arc<dyn Objective>,\n    observers: Vec<Arc<dyn TuningObserver>>,\n}\n\nimpl<S: TuningStrategy> TunerBuilder<S> {\n    fn new(strategy: S, objective: Arc<dyn Objective>) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            strategy,\n            objective,\n            observers: Vec::new(),\n        }\n    }\n\n    pub fn with_observer(mut self, observer: Arc<dyn TuningObserver>) -> Self {\n        self.observers.push(observer);\n        self\n    }\n\n    pub fn build(self) -> Tuner<S> {\n        Tuner::new(self.strategy, self.objective, self.observers)\n    }\n}\n\n/// Example objective for demonstration purposes only.\n///\n/// The objective is a noisy parabola whose global maximum is\n/// near x=0.42, y=0.69. This allows the tuner to finish quickly.\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n\n    struct DummyObjective;\n\n    impl Objective for DummyObjective {\n        fn evaluate(&self, params: &HyperParams) -> Result<f64, TuningError> {\n            let x = params.get(\"x\").copied().unwrap_or(0.0);\n            let y = params.get(\"y\").copied().unwrap_or(0.0);\n            // simple 2-D inverted paraboloid + random noise\n            let value = -(x - 0.42).powi(2) - (y - 0.69).powi(2) + 1.0\n                + rand::random::<f64>() * 0.01;\n            Ok(value)\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn random_search_converges() -> Result<(), TuningError> {\n        let mut space = ParameterSpace::new();\n        space.insert(\"x\".into(), ParameterDomain::Continuous { low: 0.0, high: 1.0 });\n        space.insert(\"y\".into(), ParameterDomain::Continuous { low: 0.0, high: 1.0 });\n\n        let strategy = RandomSearch::new(space, 1_000, 42);\n        let objective = Arc::new(DummyObjective);\n        let tracker = Arc::new(JsonlTracker::new(std::env::temp_dir().join(\"trials.jsonl\"))?);\n\n        let mut tuner = Tuner::builder(strategy, objective)\n            .with_observer(tracker)\n            .build();\n\n        let best = tuner.run()?;\n        assert!(best.score > 0.95, \"best score too low: {}\", best.score);\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn grid_search_enumerates_discrete() -> Result<(), TuningError> {\n        let mut space = ParameterSpace::new();\n        space.insert(\"x\".into(), ParameterDomain::Discrete(vec![0.0, 0.5, 1.0]));\n        space.insert(\"y\".into(), ParameterDomain::Discrete(vec![0.0, 1.0]));\n\n        let strategy = GridSearch::new(&space);\n        let objective = Arc::new(DummyObjective);\n\n        let mut tuner = Tuner::builder(strategy, objective).build();\n        let best = tuner.run()?;\n        assert!(best.score > 0.9);\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n```","numLines":395,"startLine":1,"totalLines":395}}}

  25→/// The Observer Pattern is implemented through a very small, allocation-free\n    26→/// event bus (see `EventBus` below).\n    27→#[async_trait]\n    28→pub trait TuningEventHandler: Send + Sync {\n    29→    async fn on_event(&self, event: TuningEvent);\n    30→}\n    31→\n    32→/// Enum of the events we produce while tuning.\n    33→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n    34→pub enum TuningEvent {\n    35→    TrialStarted {\n    36→        trial_id: usize,\n    37→        parameters: HyperParams,\n    38→    },\n    39→    TrialCompleted {\n    40→        trial_id:   47→        best_objective: f32,\n    48→        best_parameters: HyperParams,\n    49→        total_duration: Duration,\n    50→    },\n    51→}\n    52→\n    53→/// Very small event bus—thread-safe, allocation-friendly.\n    54→///\n    55→/// This could be swapped out for a fully-fledged pub/sub implementation\n    56→/// (Kafka, NATS, Redis Stream, etc) but is intentionally lightweight.\n    57→#[derive(Clone, Default)]\n    58→pub struct EventBus {\n    59→    /// Vector of weak references so handlers can be dropped without\n    60→    /// requiring explicit deregistration.\n    61→    handlers: Arc<Mutex<Vec<Weak<dyn TuningEventHandler>>>>,\n    62→}\n    63→\n    64→impl EventBus {\n    65→    pub fn new() -> Self {\n    66→        Self::default()\n    67→    }\n    68→\n    69→    pub fn subscribe(&self, handler: Arc<dyn TuningEventHandler>) {\n    70→        self.handlers.lock().unwrap().push(Arc::downgrade(&handler));\n    71→    }\n    72→\n    73→    async fn emit(&self, event: TuningEvent) {\n    74→        let mut stale_handlers = Vec::new();\n    75→\n    76→        for (idx, handler_weak) in self.handlers.lock().unwrap().iter().enumerate() {\n    77→            if let Some(handler_arc) = handler_weak.upgrade() {\n    78→                handler_arc.on_event(event.clone()).await;\n    79→            } else {\n    80→                stale_handlers.push(idx);\n    81→            }\n    82→        }\n    83→\n    84→        // Cleanup stale handlers\n    85→        if !stale_handlers.is_empty() {\n    86→            let mut handlers = self.handlers.lock().unwrap();\n    87→            for idx in stale_handlers.into_iter().rev() {\n    88→                handlers.swap_remove(idx);\n    89→            }\n    90→        }\n    91→    }\n    92→}\n    93→\n    94→/// Any tuner algorithm must implement this trait.\n    95→/// Strategy Pattern: different tuning algorithms share the common interface.\n    96→#[async_trait]\n    97→pub trait HyperparameterTuner: Send + Sync {\n    98→    async fn tune(&mut self) -> Result<TuningSummary, TuningError>;\n    99→}\n   100→\n   101→/// A summary of the tuning run—serializable for experiment tracking.\n   102→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n   103→pub struct TuningSummary {\n   104→    pub 111→/// Commonly raised errors during hyper-parameter tuning.\n   112→#[derive(Error, Debug)]\n   113→pub enum TuningError {\n   114→    #[error(\"invalid configuration: {0}\")]\n   115→   122→/// Enumerates the algorithms we support; can be serialized from config files.\n   123→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n   124→pub enum TuningAlgorithm {\n   125→    RandomSearch,\n   126→    GridSearch,\n   127→    // Bayesian, HyperBand, etc. can be added here\n   128→}\n   129→\n   130→impl fmt::Display for TuningAlgorithm {\n   131→    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {\n   132→        let text = match self {\n   133→            TuningAlgorithm::RandomSearch => \"random_search\",\n   134→            TuningAlgorithm::GridSearch => \"grid_search\",\n   135→        };\n   136→        write!(f, \"{text}\")\n   137→    }\n   138→}\n   139→\n   140→/// Range specification for a single hyper-parameter.\n   141→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n   142→pub enum HyperParamRange {\n   143→    Continuous { min: f32, max: f32 },\n   144→    Discrete { choices: Vec<f32> },\n   145→}\n   146→\n   147→impl HyperParamRange {\n   148→    /// Sample one value uniformly from the range.\n   149→    fn sample(&self, rng: &mut impl Rng) -> f32 {\n   150→        match self {\n   151→            HyperParamRange::Continuous { min, max } => {\n   152→                rng.sample(Uniform::new_inclusive(*min, *max))\n   153→            }\n   154→            HyperParamRange::Discrete { choices } => {\n   155→                let idx = rng.gen_range(0..choices.len());\n   156→                choices[idx]\n   157→            }\n   158→        }\n   159→    }\n   160→\n   161→    /// Generate the full grid for grid-search algorithms.\n   162→    fn full_grid(&self) -> Vec<f32> {\n   163→        match self {\n   164→            HyperParamRange::Continuous { min, max } => {\n   165→                // For simplicity, break into 10 buckets\n   166→                (0..=10)\n   167→                    .map(|i| min + ((*max - *min) * i as f32 / 10.0))\n   168→                    .collect()\n   169→            }\n   170→            HyperParamRange::Discrete { choices } => choices.clone(),\n   171→        }\n   172→    }\n   173→}\n   174→\n   175→/// A full set of hyper-parameters for one trial.  \n   176→/// Using `serde_json::Value` would be more flexible but numeric values are\n   177→/// enough for this example.\n   178→pub type HyperParams = HashMap<String, f32>;\n   179→\n   180→/// Tune-time configuration that can be read from YAML/JSON/Protobuf, etc.\n   181→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n   182→pub struct HyperparameterTuningConfig {\n   183→    pub algorithm: implementation at runtime.\n   189→pub struct TunerFactory;\n   190→\n   191→impl TunerFactory {\n   192→    pub fn build(\n   193→        cfg: HyperparameterTuningConfig,\n   194→        event_bus:   202→\n   203→/* --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   204→ * Random Search Implementation\n   205→ * ----------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n   206→\n   207→struct RandomSearchTuner {\n   208→    cfg: HyperparameterTuningConfig,\n   209→    event_bus: EventBus,\n   210→    rng: ThreadRng,\n   211→}\n   212→\n   213→impl RandomSearchTuner {\n   214→    fn new(cfg: HyperparameterTuningConfig, event_bus: EventBus) -> Self {\n   215→        Self {\n   216→            cfg,\n   217→            event_bus,\n   218→            rng: rand::thread_rng(),\n   219→        }\n   220→    }\n   221→\n   222→    fn sample_hyperparams(&mut self) -> HyperParams {\n   223→        self.cfg\n   224→            .search_space\n   225→            .iter()\n   226→            .map(|(k, v)| (k.clone(), v.sample(&mut self.rng)))\n   227→            .collect()\n   228→    }\n   229→}\n   230→\n   231→#[async_trait]\n   232→impl HyperparameterTuner for RandomSearchTuner {\n   233→    async fn tune(&mut self) -> Result<TuningSummary, TuningError> {\n   234→        let start = Instant::now();\n   235→        let mut best_objective = f32::MAX;\n   236→        let mut best_trial_id = 0;\n   237→        let mut best_params = HyperParams::new();\n   238→\n   239→        for trial_id in 0..self.cfg.max_trials {\n   240→            let params = self.sample_hyperparams();\n   241→            self.event_bus\n   242→                .emit(TuningEvent::TrialStarted {\n   243→                    trial_id,\n   244→                    parameters: params.clone(),\n   245→                })\n   246→                .await;\n   247→\n   248→            // Run the expensive objective function in a separate task so the\n   249→            // caller's async runtime remains responsive.\n   250→            let params_clone = params.clone();\n   251→            let obj_start = Instant::now();\n   252→            let objective = task::spawn_blocking(move || mock_objective_fn(&params_clone))\n   253→                .await\n   254→                .map_err(|e| .emit(TuningEvent::TrialCompleted {\n   260→                    trial_id,\n   261→                    objective,\n   262→                    duration,\n   263→                    parameters: params.clone(),\n   264→                })\n   265→                .await;\n   266→\n   267→            if objective < best_objective {\n   268→                best_objective = objective;\n   269→                best_trial_id = trial_id;\n   270→                best_params = params;\n   271→            }\n   272→        }\n   273→\n   274→        let total_time = start.elapsed();\n   275→        self.event_bus\n   276→            .emit(TuningEvent::TuningFinished {\n   277→                best_trial_id,\n   278→                best_objective,\n   279→                best_parameters: best_params.clone(),\n   280→                total_duration: total_time,\n   281→            })\n   282→            .await;\n   283→\n   284→        Ok(TuningSummary {\n   285→            best_trial_id,\n   286→            best_objective,\n   287→            best_parameters: best_params,\n   288→            trials_ran: self.cfg.max_trials,\n   289→            total_time,\n   290→        })\n   291→    }\n   292→}\n   293→\n   294→/* --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   295→ * Grid Search Implementation\n   296→ * ----------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n   297→\n   298→struct GridSearchTuner {\n   299→    cfg: HyperparameterTuningConfig,\n   300→    event_bus: EventBus,\n   301→    grid: Vec<HyperParams>,\n   302→}\n   303→\n   304→impl GridSearchTuner {\n   305→    fn new(cfg: HyperparameterTuningConfig, event_bus: EventBus) -> Self {\n   306→        let grid = build_grid(&cfg.search_space);\n   307→        Self { cfg, event_bus, grid }\n   308→    }\n   309→}\n   310→\n   311→#[async_trait]\n   312→impl HyperparameterTuner for GridSearchTuner {\n   313→    async fn tune(&mut self) -> Result<TuningSummary, TuningError> {\n   314→        if self.grid.is_empty() {\n   315→            return Err(TuningError::InvalidConfig(\n   316→                \"Grid search produced zero combinations\".into(),\n   317→            ));\n   318→        }\n   319→\n   320→        let total_trials = self.cfg.max_trials.min(self.grid.len());\n   321→        let start = Instant::now();\n   322→        let mut best_objective = f32::MAX;\n   323→        let mut best_trial_id = 0;\n   324→        let mut best_params = HyperParams::new();\n   325→\n   326→        for trial_id in 0..total_trials {\n   327→            let params = self.grid[trial_id].clone();\n   328→\n   329→            self.event_bus\n   330→                .emit(TuningEvent::TrialStarted {\n   331→                    trial_id,\n   332→                    parameters: params.clone(),\n   333→                })\n   334→                .await;\n   335→\n   336→            let obj_start = Instant::now();\n   337→            let objective = task::spawn_blocking(move || mock_objective_fn(&params))\n   338→                .await\n   339→                .map_err(|e| TuningError::Internal(e.to_string()))?;\n   340→            let duration = obj_start.elapsed();\n   341→\n   342→            self.event_bus\n   343→                .emit(TuningEvent::TrialCompleted {\n   344→                    trial_id,\n   345→                    objective,\n   346→                    duration,\n   347→                    parameters: params.clone(),\n   348→                })\n   349→                .await;\n   350→\n   351→            if objective < best_objective {\n   352→                best_objective = objective;\n   353→                best_trial_id = trial_id;\n   354→                best_params = params.clone();\n   355→            }\n   356→        }\n   357→\n   358→        let total_time = start.elapsed();\n   359→        self.event_bus\n   360→            .emit(TuningEvent::TuningFinished {\n   361→                best_trial_id,\n   362→                best_objective,\n   363→                best_parameters: best_params.clone(),\n   364→                total_duration: total_time,\n   365→            })\n   366→            .await;\n   367→\n   368→        Ok(TuningSummary {\n   369→            best_trial_id,\n   370→            best_objective,\n   371→            best_parameters: best_params,\n   372→            trials_ran: total_trials,\n   373→            total_time,\n   374→        })\n   375→    }\n   376→}\n   377→\n   378→/* --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   379→ * Helper Functions\n   380→ * ----------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n   381→\n   382→/// Mock objective function—replace with real model training + validation.\n   383→/// Lower is better.\n   384→fn mock_objective_fn(params: &HyperParams) -> f32 {\n   385→    // Deterministic-ish “objective” so unit tests can assert repeatably.\n   386→    params.values().fold(0.0, |acc, v| acc + (v * v)).sqrt()\n   387→}\n   388→\n   389→/// Build a Cartesian product of all parameter ranges.\n   390→/// For large spaces consider lazy evaluation or iterators.\n   391→fn build_grid(search_space: &HashMap<String, HyperParamRange>) -> Vec<HyperParams> {\n   392→    fn cartesian(\n   393→        keys: &[String],\n   394→        search_space: &HashMap<String, HyperParamRange>,\n   395→        idx: usize,\n   396→        current: &mut HyperParams,\n   397→        grid: &mut Vec<HyperParams>,\n   398→    ) {\n   399→        if idx == keys.len() {\n   400→            grid.push(current.clone());\n   401→            return;\n   402→        }\n   403→\n   404→        let key = &keys[idx];\n   405→        for val in search_space[key].full_grid() {\n   406→            current.insert(key.clone(), val);\n   407→            cartesian(keys, search_space, idx + 1, current, grid);\n   408→        }\n   409→        current.remove(key);\n   410→    }\n   411→\n   412→    let keys: Vec<String> = search_space.keys().cloned().collect();\n   413→    let mut grid = Vec::new();\n   414→    let mut current = HyperParams::new();\n   415→    cartesian(&keys, search_space, 0, &mut current, &mut grid);\n   416→    grid\n   417→}\n   418→\n   419→/* Hyper-parameter tuning utilities used by VisuTility Orchestrator\n//!\n//! This module demonstrates the Strategy, Factory, and Observer patterns\n//! to provide pluggable hyper-parameter tuning back-ends while emitting\n//! structured events that can be consumed by experiment trackers,\n//! dashboards, or automated re-training triggers.\n\nuse std::{\n    collections::HashMap,\n    fmt,\n    sync::{Arc, Mutex, Weak},\n    time::{Duration, Instant},\n};\n\nuse async_trait::async_trait;\nuse rand::{distributions::Uniform, prelude::*};\nuse serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\nuse thiserror::Error;\nuse tokio::task;\n\n/// Anything in the platform that wants to be informed about tuning progress\n/// implements this trait.  \n/// The Observer Pattern is implemented through a very small, allocation-free\n/// event bus (see `EventBus` below).\n#[async_trait]\npub trait TuningEventHandler: Send + Sync {\n    async fn on_event(&self, event: TuningEvent);\n}\n\n/// Enum of the events we produce while tuning.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub enum TuningEvent {\n    TrialStarted {\n        trial_id: usize,\n        parameters: HyperParams,\n    },\n    TrialCompleted {\n        trial_id: usize,\n        objective: f32,\n        duration: Duration,\n        parameters: HyperParams,\n    },\n    TuningFinished {\n        best_trial_id: usize,\n        best_objective: f32,\n        best_parameters: HyperParams,\n        total_duration: Duration,\n    },\n}\n\n/// Very small event bus—thread-safe, allocation-friendly.\n///\n/// This could be swapped out for a fully-fledged pub/sub implementation\n/// (Kafka, NATS, Redis Stream, etc) but is intentionally lightweight.\n#[derive(Clone, Default)]\npub struct EventBus {\n    /// Vector of weak references so handlers can be dropped without\n    /// requiring explicit deregistration.\n    handlers: Arc<Mutex<Vec<Weak<dyn TuningEventHandler>>>>,\n}\n\nimpl EventBus {\n    pub fn new() -> Self {\n        Self::default()\n    }\n\n    pub fn subscribe(&self, handler: Arc<dyn TuningEventHandler>) {\n        self.handlers.lock().unwrap().push(Arc::downgrade(&handler));\n    }\n\n    async fn emit(&self, event: TuningEvent) {\n        let mut stale_handlers = Vec::new();\n\n        for (idx, handler_weak) in self.handlers.lock().unwrap().iter().enumerate() {\n            if let Some(handler_arc) = handler_weak.upgrade() {\n                handler_arc.on_event(event.clone()).await;\n            } else {\n                stale_handlers.push(idx);\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Cleanup stale handlers\n        if !stale_handlers.is_empty() {\n            let mut handlers = self.handlers.lock().unwrap();\n            for idx in stale_handlers.into_iter().rev() {\n                handlers.swap_remove(idx);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Any tuner algorithm must implement this trait.\n/// Strategy Pattern: different tuning algorithms share the common interface.\n#[async_trait]\npub trait HyperparameterTuner: Send + Sync {\n    async fn tune(&mut self) -> Result<TuningSummary, TuningError>;\n}\n\n/// A summary of the tuning run—serializable for experiment tracking.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct TuningSummary {\n    pub best_trial_id: usize,\n    pub best_objective: f32,\n    pub best_parameters: HyperParams,\n    pub trials_ran: usize,\n    pub total_time: Duration,\n}\n\n/// Commonly raised errors during hyper-parameter tuning.\n#[derive(Error, Debug)]\npub enum TuningError {\n    #[error(\"invalid configuration: {0}\")]\n    InvalidConfig(String),\n    #[error(\"I/O error: {0}\")]\n    Io(#[from] std::io::Error),\n    #[error(\"internal error: {0}\")]\n    Internal(String),\n}\n\n/// Enumerates the algorithms we support; can be serialized from config files.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub enum TuningAlgorithm {\n    RandomSearch,\n    GridSearch,\n    // Bayesian, HyperBand, etc. can be added here\n}\n\nimpl fmt::Display for TuningAlgorithm {\n    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {\n        let text = match self {\n            TuningAlgorithm::RandomSearch => \"random_search\",\n            TuningAlgorithm::GridSearch => \"grid_search\",\n        };\n        write!(f, \"{text}\")\n    }\n}\n\n/// Range specification for a single hyper-parameter.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub enum HyperParamRange {\n    Continuous { min: f32, max: f32 },\n    Discrete { choices: Vec<f32> },\n}\n\nimpl HyperParamRange {\n    /// Sample one value uniformly from the range.\n    fn sample(&self, rng: &mut impl Rng) -> f32 {\n        match self {\n            HyperParamRange::Continuous { min, max } => {\n                rng.sample(Uniform::new_inclusive(*min, *max))\n            }\n            HyperParamRange::Discrete { choices } => {\n                let idx = rng.gen_range(0..choices.len());\n                choices[idx]\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Generate the full grid for grid-search algorithms.\n    fn full_grid(&self) -> Vec<f32> {\n        match self {\n            HyperParamRange::Continuous { min, max } => {\n                // For simplicity, break into 10 buckets\n                (0..=10)\n                    .map(|i| min + ((*max - *min) * i as f32 / 10.0))\n                    .collect()\n            }\n            HyperParamRange::Discrete { choices } => choices.clone(),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// A full set of hyper-parameters for one trial.  \n/// Using `serde_json::Value` would be more flexible but numeric values are\n/// enough for this example.\npub type HyperParams = HashMap<String, f32>;\n\n/// Tune-time configuration that can be read from YAML/JSON/Protobuf, etc.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct HyperparameterTuningConfig {\n    pub algorithm: TuningAlgorithm,\n    pub search_space: HashMap<String, HyperParamRange>,\n    pub max_trials: usize,\n}\n\n/// Factory Pattern: pick the correct strategy implementation at runtime.\npub struct TunerFactory;\n\nimpl TunerFactory {\n    pub fn build(\n        cfg: HyperparameterTuningConfig,\n        event_bus: EventBus,\n    ) -> Result<Box<dyn HyperparameterTuner>, TuningError> {\n        match cfg.algorithm {\n            TuningAlgorithm::RandomSearch => Ok(Box::new(RandomSearchTuner::new(cfg, event_bus))),\n            TuningAlgorithm::GridSearch => Ok(Box::new(GridSearchTuner::new(cfg, event_bus))),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/* --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Random Search Implementation\n * ----------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nstruct RandomSearchTuner {\n    cfg: HyperparameterTuningConfig,\n    event_bus: EventBus,\n    rng: ThreadRng,\n}\n\nimpl RandomSearchTuner {\n    fn new(cfg: HyperparameterTuningConfig, event_bus: EventBus) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            cfg,\n            event_bus,\n            rng: rand::thread_rng(),\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn sample_hyperparams(&mut self) -> HyperParams {\n        self.cfg\n            .search_space\n            .iter()\n            .map(|(k, v)| (k.clone(), v.sample(&mut self.rng)))\n            .collect()\n    }\n}\n\n#[async_trait]\nimpl HyperparameterTuner for RandomSearchTuner {\n    async fn tune(&mut self) -> Result<TuningSummary, TuningError> {\n        let start = Instant::now();\n        let mut best_objective = f32::MAX;\n        let mut best_trial_id = 0;\n        let mut best_params = HyperParams::new();\n\n        for trial_id in 0..self.cfg.max_trials {\n            let params = self.sample_hyperparams();\n            self.event_bus\n                .emit(TuningEvent::TrialStarted {\n                    trial_id,\n                    parameters: params.clone(),\n                })\n                .await;\n\n            // Run the expensive objective function in a separate task so the\n            // caller's async runtime remains responsive.\n            let params_clone = params.clone();\n            let obj_start = Instant::now();\n            let objective = task::spawn_blocking(move || mock_objective_fn(&params_clone))\n                .await\n                .map_err(|e| TuningError::Internal(e.to_string()))?;\n\n            let duration = obj_start.elapsed();\n\n            self.event_bus\n                .emit(TuningEvent::TrialCompleted {\n                    trial_id,\n                    objective,\n                    duration,\n                    parameters: params.clone(),\n                })\n                .await;\n\n            if objective < best_objective {\n                best_objective = objective;\n                best_trial_id = trial_id;\n                best_params = params;\n            }\n        }\n\n        let total_time = best_params.clone(),\n                total_duration: total_time,\n            })\n            .await;\n\n        Ok(TuningSummary {\n            best_trial_id,\n            best_objective,\n            best_parameters: best_params,\n            trials_ran: self.cfg.max_trials,\n            total_time,\n        })\n    }\n}\n\n/* --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Grid Search Implementation\n * ----------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nstruct GridSearchTuner {\n    cfg: HyperparameterTuningConfig,\n    event_bus: EventBus,\n    grid: Vec<HyperParams>,\n}\n\nimpl GridSearchTuner {\n    fn new(cfg: HyperparameterTuningConfig, event_bus: EventBus) -> Self {\n        let grid = build_grid(&cfg.search_space);\n        Self { cfg, event_bus, grid }\n    }\n}\n\n#[async_trait]\nimpl HyperparameterTuner for GridSearchTuner {\n    async fn tune(&mut self) -> Result<TuningSummary, TuningError> {\n        if self.grid.is_empty() {\n            return Err(TuningError::InvalidConfig(\n                \"Grid search produced zero combinations\".into(),\n            ));\n        }\n\n        let total_trials = self.cfg.max_trials.min(self.grid.len());\n        let start = Instant::now();\n        let mut best_objective = f32::MAX;\n        let mut best_trial_id = 0;\n        let mut best_params = HyperParams::new();\n\n        for trial_id in 0..total_trials {\n            let params = self.grid[trial_id].clone();\n\n            self.event_bus\n                .emit(TuningEvent::TrialStarted {\n                    trial_id,\n                    parameters: params.clone(),\n                })\n                .await;\n\n            let obj_start = Instant::now();\n            let objective = task::spawn_blocking(move || mock_objective_fn(&params))\n                .await\n                .map_err(|e| TuningError::Internal(e.to_string()))?;\n            let duration = obj_start.elapsed();\n\n            self.event_bus\n                .emit(TuningEvent::TrialCompleted {\n                    trial_id,\n                    objective,\n                    duration,\n                    parameters: params.clone(),\n                })\n                .await;\n\n            if objective < best_objective {\n                best_objective = objective;\n                best_trial_id = trial_id;\n                best_params = params.clone();\n            }\n        }\n\n        let total_time = start.elapsed();\n        self.event_bus\n            .emit(TuningEvent::TuningFinished {\n                best_trial_id,\n                best_objective,\n                best_parameters: best_params.clone(),\n                total_duration: total_time,\n            })\n            .await;\n\n        Ok(TuningSummary {\n            best_trial_id,\n            best_objective,\n            best_parameters: best_params,\n            trials_ran: total_trials,\n            total_time,\n        })\n    }\n}\n\n/* --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Helper Functions\n * ----------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/// Mock objective function—replace with real model training + validation.\n/// Lower is better.\nfn mock_objective_fn(params: &HyperParams) -> f32 {\n    // Deterministic-ish “objective” so unit tests can assert repeatably.\n    params.values().fold(0.0, |acc, v| acc + (v * v)).sqrt()\n}\n\n/// Build a Cartesian product of all parameter ranges.\n/// For large spaces consider lazy evaluation or iterators.\nfn build_grid(search_space: &HashMap<String, HyperParamRange>) -> Vec<HyperParams> {\n    fn cartesian(\n        keys: &[String],\n        search_space: &HashMap<String, HyperParamRange>,\n        idx: usize,\n        current: &mut HyperParams,\n        grid: &mut Vec<HyperParams>,\n    ) {\n        if idx == keys.len() {\n            grid.push(current.clone());\n            return;\n        }\n\n        let key = &keys[idx];\n        for val in search_space[key].full_grid() {\n            current.insert(key.clone(), val);\n            cartesian(keys, search_space, idx + 1, current, grid);\n        }\n        current.remove(key);\n    }\n\n    let keys: Vec<String> = search_space.keys().cloned().collect();\n    let mut grid = Vec::new();\n    let mut current = HyperParams::new();\n    cartesian(&keys, search_space, 0, &mut current, &mut grid);\n    grid\n}\n\n/* --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Example Observer—writes events to Module 54 − Hyper-parameter tuning + metric-based retraining trigger.\n//!\n//! This module demonstrates how VisuTility Orchestrator wires together\n//! The code is intentionally self-contained but designed to integrate\n//! with the wider orchestrator through trait-based interfaces and the\n//! internal event bus.\n\nuse std::{\n self.params.insert(key.into(), value);\n    }\n\n    pub fn get(&self, key: &str) -> Option<f64> {\n        self.params.get(key).cloned()\n    }\n}\n\n/// Training (or validation) metrics used as    /// Given an initial configuration and the latest metrics, propose a new config.\n    fn tune(\n        &mut self,\n        last_config: &HyperParameterConfig,\n        last_metrics: &TrainingMetrics,\n    ) -> TunerResult<HyperParameterConfig>;\n}\n\n/// Naïve grid-search tuning strategy.\npub struct GridSearchTuner {\n    /// Step size applied to each parameter during search.\n strategy_name(&self) -> &'static str {\n        \"grid_search\"\n    }\n\n    fn tune(\n        &mut self,\n        last_config: &HyperParameterConfig,\n        _last_metrics: &TrainingMetrics,\n    ) -> TunerResult<HyperParameterConfig> {\n        // Very small example: bump each param by `step_size`\n        let mut new_cfg = last_config.clone();\n        for value in new_cfg.params.values_mut() {\n            *value += self.step_size;\n        }\n        Ok(new_cfg)\n    }\n}\n\n/// Very light-weight pseudo-Bayesian tuner mock (random exploration).\npub struct BayesianTuner {\n    rng: ThreadRng,\n    exploration_rate: f64,\n}\n\nimpl BayesianTuner {\n    pub fn new(exploration_rate: f64) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            rng: thread_rng(),\n            exploration_rate,\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl HyperparameterTuner for BayesianTuner {\n    fn strategy_name(&self) -> &'static str {\n        \"bayesian_search\"\n    }\n\n    fn tune(\n        &mut self,\n        last_config: &HyperParameterConfig,\n        _last_metrics: &TrainingMetrics,\n    ) -> TunerResult<HyperParameterConfig> {\n        let mut new_cfg = last_config.clone();\n        for value in new_cfg.params.values_mut() {\n            let noise: f64 = self.rng.gen::<f64>() * self.exploration_rate;\n            *value += noise - (self.exploration_rate / 2.0);\n        }\n        Ok(new_cfg)\n    }\n}\n\n//\n// ---------- Factory Pattern: TunerFactory --------------------------------------------------------\n//\n\n/// Available tuning strategy identifiers.\n#[derive(Debug, TunerKind::Bayesian => Box::new(BayesianTuner::new(0.10)),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n//\n// ---------- Observer Pattern: PerformanceMonitor -------------------------------------------------\n//\n\n/// tx_retrain,\n            model_id,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Computes moving average loss over the internal window.\n    fn avg_loss(&self) -> Option<f32> {\n        if self.history.is_empty() {\n            return None;\n        }\n        Some(\n            self.history\n                .iter()\n                .map(|m| m.loss)\n                .sum::<f32>()\n                / self.history.len() as f32,\n        )\n    }\n}\n\nimpl MetricsObserver for PerformanceMonitor {\n    fn on_metrics_update(&mut self, metrics: &TrainingMetrics) {\n        // Maintain bounded window.\n        if self.history.len() == self.window {\n            self.history.pop_front();\n        }\n        self.history.push_back(*metrics);\n\n        // Compute rolling avg and detect degradation.\n        if let Some(avg_loss) = self.avg_loss() {\n            let worst_allowed = avg_loss * (1.0 + self.threshold_pct);\n            if metrics.loss > worst_allowed {\n                // Push retrain event.\n                let evt = RetrainEvent {\n                    model_id: self.model_id.clone(),\n                    reason: format!(\n                        \"Loss degraded {:.2}% vs. rolling mean\",\n                        (metrics.loss / avg_loss - 1.0) * 100.0\n                    ),\n                };\n                // It's okay to ignore send errors — they indicate closed consumer.\n                let _ = self.tx_retrain.send(evt);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n//\n// ---------- Orchestration Boilerplate likely run in a Tokio\n/// executor in the real orchestrator; here we model it with plain threads\n/// for minimal dependencies.\n///\n/// The loop:\n/// 1. Consumes new metrics from `rx_metrics`.\n/// 2. Feeds them to the tuner.\n/// 3. Broadcasts them to observers.\n/// 4. {\n                    recv(self.rx_metrics) -> msg => {\n                        match msg {\n                            Ok((cfg, metrics)) => {\n                                // 1) Produce new config via tuner.\n                                let mut tuner = self.tuner.lock().expect(\"poisoned lock\");\n                                match tuner.tune(&cfg, &metrics) {\n                                    Ok(new_cfg) => {\n                                        // Send back to upstream training service\n                                        if let Err(err) = self.tx_config.send(new_cfg) {\n                                            eprintln!(\"TuningCoordinator: failed to send new config: {err}\");\n                                        }\n                                    }\n                                    Err(e) => eprintln!(\"TuningCoordinator: tune() failed: {e:?}\"),\n                                }\n\n                                // 2) Notify observers.\n                                for obs in self.observers.lock().expect(\"poisoned lock\").iter_mut() {\n                                    obs.on_metrics_update(&metrics);\n                                }\n                            }\n                            Err(_) => {\n                                // Upstream closed. Shutdown gracefully.\n                                break;\n                            }\n                        }\n                    }\n                    recv(ticker) -> _ => {\n                        // Heart-beat for logging / metrics integration.\n                        // In prod we would emit metrics to Prometheus here.\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        });\n    }\n}\n\n//\n// ---------- Self-contained Integration Test -------------------------------------------------------\n//\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n\n    #[test]\n    fn end_to_end_flow() {\n        // a) Wire channels.\n        let (tx_metrics, rx_metrics) = bounded::<(HyperParameterConfig, TrainingMetrics)>(4);\n        let (tx_cfg_out, rx_cfg_out) = bounded::<HyperParameterConfig>(4);\n        let (tx_retrain, _rx_retrain) = bounded::<RetrainEvent>(4);\n\n        // b) Concrete strategy + observer.\n        let tuner = TunerFactory::create(TunerKind::GridSearch);\n        let monitor = PerformanceMonitor::new(\"model-xyz\".into(), 4, 0.05, tx_retrain);\n\n        // c) Coordinator.\n        let coord = TuningCoordinator::new(tuner, vec![Box::new(monitor)], rx_metrics, tx_cfg_out);\n        coord.start();\n\n        // d) Push dummy metrics.\n        let mut cfg = HyperParameterConfig::new();\n        cfg.insert(\"lr\", 0.01);\n        cfg.insert(\"momentum\", 0.9);\n\n        for i in 0..5 {\n            let mut metrics = TrainingMetrics {\n                accuracy: 0.9,\n                f1_score: 0.92,\n                loss: 0.1 + i as f32 * 0.02,\n            };\n            tx_metrics.send((cfg.clone(), metrics)).unwrap();\n\n            // Receive tuned config.\n            let new_cfg = rx_cfg_out.recv().unwrap();\n            assert!(new_cfg.get(\"lr\").is_some());\n        }\n    }\n}\n```","numLines":396,"startLine":1,"totalLines":396}}}

Model-Monitoring & Automated-Retraining Orchestrator\n     5→//! --------------------------------------------------------------\n     6→//! This module wires together the Observer & Strategy patterns used by the\n     7→//! VisuTility Orchestrator to\n     8→//!   1. observe live model-serving metrics,\n     9→//!   2. detect statistical drift or performance regressions, and\n    10→//!   3. trigger an automated retraining job through the internal MLOps\n    11→//!      pipeline when necessary.\n    12→//!\n    13→//! All of those\n    15→//! concerns are injected via trait objects, keeping the module easily\n    16→//! testable and reusable across binaries and workspaces.\n    17→\n    18→use 24→\n    25→/// A normalized metric payload emitted by the Serving-Ops layer.\n    26→///\n    27→/// Requirements:\n    28→///   • `value` MUST be normalized so that higher is always better.  \n    29→///   • `timestamp` must be captured close to the model to guarantee\n    30→///     time-series fidelity even in presence of network jitter.\n    31→#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n    32→pub struct ShouldRetrain,\n    58→}\n    59→\n    60→/// Strategy that evaluates a sliding window of metrics and decides whether\n    61→/// automated retraining should be triggered.\n    62→///\n    63→/// Implementations MUST be thread-safe and cheap to clone: they are wrapped\n    64→/// in `Arc` internally.\n    65→pub trait RetrainingStrategy: Send + Sync + 'static {\n    66→    fn evaluate(\n    67→        &self,\n    68→        metric_name: &str,\n    69→        window: &[MetricSample],\n    70→    ) -> Result<RetrainingDecision, MonitorError>;\n    71→}\n    72→\n    73→/// A For an F1-score in [0,1] you may want to trigger retraining once the\n    78→///   average score of the last 10 samples falls under 0.80.\n    79→pub struct StatisticalAverageStrategy {\n    80→ Model-Monitoring & Automated-Retraining Orchestrator\n//! --------------------------------------------------------------\n//! This module wires together the Observer & Strategy patterns used by the\n//! VisuTility Orchestrator to\n//!   1. observe live model-serving metrics,\n//!   2. detect statistical drift or performance regressions, and\n//!   3. trigger an automated retraining job through the internal MLOps\n//!      pipeline when necessary.\n//!\n//! It is fully self-contained and agnostic of the concrete metric backend,\n//! storage layer, or experiment-tracking implementation.  All of those\n//! concerns are injected via trait objects, keeping the module easily\n//! testable and reusable across binaries and workspaces.\n\nuse std::collections::VecDeque;\nuse std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};\nuse std::time::{Duration, Instant};\n\nuse log::{debug, error, info, warn};\nuse thiserror::Error;\n\n/// A normalized metric payload emitted by the Serving-Ops layer.\n///\n/// Requirements:\n///   • `value` MUST be normalized so that higher is always better.  \n///   • `timestamp` must be captured close to the model to guarantee\n///     time-series fidelity even in presence of network jitter.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct MetricSample {\n    pub metric_name: String,\n    pub value: f64,\n    pub timestamp: Instant,\n}\n\n/// Errors surfaced by the Model Monitor and its collaborators.\n#[derive(Debug, Error)]\npub enum MonitorError {\n    #[error(\"observer failed: {0}\")]\n    ObserverFailure(String),\n    #[error(\"strategy evaluation failed: {0}\")]\n    StrategyFailure(String),\n}\n\n/// Trait implemented by components interested in real-time metric updates\n/// (dashboards, alarm systems, audit logs, …).\npub trait MetricObserver: Send + Sync + 'static {\n    fn on_metric(&self, sample: &MetricSample) -> Result<(), MonitorError>;\n}\n\n/// Decision returned by a retraining strategy after evaluating a metric window.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]\npub enum RetrainingDecision {\n    NoAction,\n    ShouldRetrain,\n}\n\n/// Strategy that evaluates a sliding window of metrics and decides whether\n/// automated retraining should be triggered.\n///\n/// Implementations MUST be thread-safe and cheap to clone: they are wrapped\n/// in `Arc` internally.\npub trait RetrainingStrategy: Send + Sync + 'static {\n    fn evaluate(\n        &self,\n        metric_name: &str,\n        window: &[MetricSample],\n    ) -> Result<RetrainingDecision, MonitorError>;\n}\n\n/// A statistically-driven strategy that triggers retraining when the average\n/// of the last `window_size` samples drops below `min_acceptable_score`.\n///\n/// Example:\n///   For an F1-score in [0,1] you may want to trigger retraining once the\n///   average score of the last 10 samples falls under 0.80.\npub struct StatisticalAverageStrategy {\n    min_acceptable_score: f64,\n    window_size: usize,\n}\n\nimpl StatisticalAverageStrategy {\n    pub fn new(min_acceptable_score: f64, window_size: usize) -> Self {\n        assert!(\n            (0.0..=1.0).contains(&min_acceptable_score),\n            \"min_acceptable_score must be in [0,1]\"\n        );\n        assert!(window_size > 1, \"window_size must be > 1\");\n\n        Self {\n            min_acceptable_score,\n            window_size,\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl RetrainingStrategy for StatisticalAverageStrategy {\n    fn evaluate(\n        &self,\n        _metric_name: &str,\n        window: &[MetricSample],\n    ) -> Result<RetrainingDecision, MonitorError> {\n        if window.len() < self.window_size {\n            return Ok(RetrainingDecision::NoAction);\n        }\n        let avg: f64 = window\n            .iter()\n            .rev()\n            .take(self.window_size)\n            .map(|s| s.value)\n            .sum::<f64>()\n            / self.window_size as f64;\n\n        debug!(\n            \"[Strategy::StatisticalAverage] avg={:.4} threshold={:.4}\",\n            avg, self.min_acceptable_score\n        );\n\n        if avg < self.min_acceptable_score {\n            Ok(RetrainingDecision::ShouldRetrain)\n        } else {\n            Ok(RetrainingDecision::NoAction)\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Central coordinator that\n///   • accumulates metric samples in a ring buffer,\n///   • notifies observers,\n///   • delegates to strategies for retraining decisions.\n///\n#[derive(Clone)]\npub struct ModelMonitor {\n    inner: Arc<Mutex<Inner>>,\n}\n\nstruct Inner {\n    window: VecDeque<MetricSample>,\n    max_window: usize,\n    observers: Vec<Arc<dyn MetricObserver>>,\n    strategies: Vec<Arc<dyn RetrainingStrategy>>,\n    cooldown: Duration,\n    last_retrain_at: Option<Instant>,\n}\n\nimpl ModelMonitor {\n    /// Create a new monitor.\n    ///\n    /// `max_window`  – maximum number of samples kept per metric  \n    /// `cooldown`    – minimum delay between two retraining triggers\n    pub fn new(max_window: usize, cooldown: Duration) -> Self {\n        assert!(max_window > 1, \"max_window must be > 1\");\n\n        Self {\n            inner: Arc::new(Mutex::new(Inner {\n                window: VecDeque::with_capacity(max_window),\n                max_window,\n                observers: Vec::new(),\n                strategies: Vec::new(),\n                cooldown,\n                last_retrain_at: None,\n            })),\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Register a new observer.  Failures in an observer do not abort the\n    /// ingest flow; they are logged and swallowed.\n    pub fn register_observer<O>(&self, observer: O)\n    where\n        O: MetricObserver,\n    {\n        self.inner.lock().unwrap().observers.push(Arc::new(observer));\n    }\n\n    /// Register a new retraining strategy.  The first strategy voting for\n    /// `ShouldRetrain` short-circuits evaluation of further strategies.\n    pub fn register_strategy<S>(&self, strategy: S)\n    where\n        S: RetrainingStrategy,\n    {\n        self.inner.lock().unwrap().strategies.push(Arc::new(strategy));\n    }\n\n    /// Ingest a single metric sample into the monitor.\n    ///\n    /// Return `Ok(true)`  if a retraining event was emitted.  \n    /// Return `Ok(false)` if no action was taken.  \n    /// Return `Err(_)`    if a strategy returned an unrecoverable error.\n    pub fn ingest_metric(&self, sample: MetricSample) -> Result<bool, MonitorError> {\n        let mut inner = self.inner.lock().unwrap();\n\n        // 1) add sample to ring buffer\n        if inner.window.len() == inner.max_window {\n            inner.window.pop_front();\n        }\n        inner.window.push_back(sample.clone());\n\n        // 2) notify observers\n        for obs in &inner.observers {\n            if let Err(e) = obs.on_metric(&sample) {\n                error!(\"observer failed: {}\", e);\n            }\n        }\n\n        // 3) consult each strategy\n        for strategy in &inner.strategies {\n            match strategy.evaluate(&sample.metric_name, &inner.window) {\n                Ok(RetrainingDecision::ShouldRetrain) => {\n                    let now = Instant::now();\n                    if inner\n                        .last_retrain_at\n                        .map_or(true, |t| now.duration_since(t) >= inner.cooldown)\n                    {\n                        inner.last_retrain_at = Some(now);\n                        info!(\n                            \"Retraining triggered for metric '{}' (cooldown = {:?})\",\n                            sample.metric_name, inner.cooldown\n                        );\n                        return Ok(true);\n                    } else {\n                        warn!(\"Retraining skipped due to cooldown.\");\n                        return Ok(false);\n                    }\n                }\n                Ok(RetrainingDecision::NoAction) => {\n                    // continue with next strategy\n                }\n                Err(e) => return Err(e),\n            }\n        }\n\n        Ok(false)\n    }\n}\n\n/// A trivial console observer handy for local debugging.\npub struct *self.counter.lock().unwrap() += 1;\n            Ok(())\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn retraining_is_triggered_when_average_drops() {\n        let monitor = ModelMonitor::new(10, Duration::from_secs(1));\n        monitor.register_strategy(StatisticalAverageStrategy::new(0.8, 5));\n\n        // push 5 good samples\n        for _ in 0..5 {\n            assert!(!monitor\n                .ingest_metric(MetricSample {\n                    metric_name: \"f1\".into(),\n                    value: 0.9,\n                    timestamp: Instant::now(),\n                })\n                .unwrap());\n        }\n\n        // push 5 degraded samples -> avg below threshold -> retrain\n        let mut triggered = false;\n        for _ in 0..5 {\n            triggered = monitor\n                .ingest_metric(MetricSample {\n                    metric_name: \"f1\".into(),\n                    value: 0.5,\n                    timestamp: Instant::now(),\n                })\n                .unwrap();\n        }\n        assert!(triggered);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn observer_is_called_for_each_sample() {\n        let monitor = ModelMonitor::new(5, Duration::from_secs(60));\n        let counter = Arc::new(Mutex::new(0));\n        monitor.register_observer(CountingObserver::new(counter.clone()));\n\n        for _ in 0..3 {\n            monitor\n                .ingest_metric(MetricSample {\n                    metric_name: \"latency_ms\".into(),\n                    value: 15.0,\n                    timestamp: Instant::now(),\n                })\n                .unwrap();\n        }\n\n        assert_eq!(*counter.lock().unwrap(), 3);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn cooldown_is_enforced_between_triggers() {\n        let monitor = ModelMonitor::new(10, Duration::from_millis(300));\n        monitor.register_strategy(StatisticalAverageStrategy::new(0.8, 1));\n\n        // first trigger should fire\n        assert!(monitor\n            .ingest_metric(MetricSample {\n                metric_name: \"accuracy\".into(),\n                value: 0.0,\n                timestamp: Instant::now(),\n            })\n            .unwrap());\n\n 0.0,\n                timestamp: Instant::now(),\n            })\n            .unwrap());\n\n        thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(400));\n\n        // after cooldown passes, trigger should fire Core Component Identification\n\nThe experiment tracking functionality is distributed across multiple modules following a consistent architectural pattern:\n\n| Module | Primary Responsibility | Key Types |\n|--------|----------------------|-----------|\n| `module_12.txt` | In-memory experiment run management | `ExperimentTracker`, `ExperimentRun`, `TrainingEvent` |\n| `module_25.txt` | Tracker trait and factory | `ExperimentTracker` (trait), `FileTracker`, `HttpTracker`, `TrackerFactory` |\n| `module_20.txt` | Tracker trait and file-based logging | `ExperimentTracker` (trait), `LocalFileTracker`, `TrialFinishedEvent` |\n| `module_71.txt` | SQLite-backed persistence | `ExperimentTracker`, `ExperimentRecord` |\n| `module_55.txt` | Observer pattern for experiments | `ExperimentEvent`, `ExperimentObserver`, `TrialRecord` |\n| `module_48.txt` | JSONL file-based observer | `TuningObserver`, `JsonlTracker` |\n| `module_76.txt` | Monitoring integration | `ExperimentTracker` (for retraining logs) |\n\n### 2. Dependency Analysis\n\n#### Internal Dependencies (what core modules depend on):\n\n| Dependency | Modules Using It | Purpose |\n|------------|-----------------|---------|\n| `config.txt` | All trackers | `experiment_tracking_uri`, backend selection |\n| `utils.txt` | All modules | `AppConfig`, `event_bus`, error types, `ScopedTimer` |\n| `constants.txt` | Feature-flagged modules | `HYPERPARAM_TUNING` feature flag |\n| `serde`, `serde_json` | All trackers | Serialization of trials and metrics |\n| `tokio`, `async_trait` | Async trackers | Async I/O operations |\n| `rusqlite` | `module_71.txt` | SQLite persistence |\n| `reqwest` | `module_25.txt` | HTTP-based remote tracking |\n\n#### External Consumers (modules that use experiment tracking):\n\n| Consumer Module | How It Uses Experiment Tracking |\n|-----------------|--------------------------------|\n| `module_77.txt` | `EventBus` emits `TuningEvent` for experiment logging |\n| `module_54.txt` | `TuningCoordinator` feeds metrics to observers |\n| `module_43.txt` | `ModelMonitor` triggers retraining with metric logging |\n| `module_76.txt` | `RetrainTrigger` records experiments after tuning |\n| `module_20.txt` | `HyperparameterRunner` logs trials via tracker list |\n| `module_53.txt` | Tuning strategies log results |\n| `module_23.txt` | Factory creates tuners that integrate with trackers |\n\n### 3. Architectural Patterns Used\n\n1. **Strategy Pattern**: Multiple tuning algorithms (`GridSearchTuner`, `RandomSearchTuner`, `BayesianTuner`) implement common `Tuner`/`HyperparameterTuner` traits\n2. **Factory Pattern**: `TrackerFactory`, `TunerFactory` instantiate concrete implementations at runtime\n3. **Observer Pattern**: `ExperimentObserver`, `TrainingObserver`, `TuningObserver` receive notifications about trial events\n4. **Repository Pattern**: `ExperimentTracker` abstracts persistence (file, HTTP, SQLite backends)\n\n## Proposed Service API\n\n### RESTful API Design for Experiment
