Direct in-memory `ExperimentTracker` usage | Replace with HTTP client calls to `POST /runs`, `POST /metrics`, `PATCH /runs/{id}` |\n| `module_20.txt` | `HyperparameterRunner` calls `trackers.log_trial()` directly | Inject `ExperimentTrackingClient` that makes HTTP calls instead of direct trait calls |\n| `module_25.txt` | Contains `FileTracker`, `HttpTracker`, `TrackerFactory` | Remove tracker implementations; keep only a thin HTTP client implementing the `ExperimentTracker` trait |\n| `module_54.txt` | `TuningCoordinator` notifies internal observers | Replace internal observer notifications with API calls to log metrics |\n| `module_55.txt` | `ExperimentObserver` receives `ExperimentEvent` internally | Observer implementations call HTTP endpoints instead of local logging |\n| `module_71.txt` | SQLite-backed `ExperimentTracker` persistence | Remove SQLite code; replace with API client calls |\n| `module_76.txt` | `RetrainTrigger` uses `tracker.record()` | Replace with `POST /experiments/{id}/runs` and metric logging calls |\n| `module_77.txt` | `EventBus.emit()` for `TuningEvent` | Add HTTP client that translates `TuningEvent` to API calls |\n| `module_43.txt` | `ModelMonitor` with internal metric tracking | Metrics ingestion should forward to experiment tracking API |\n| `module_48.txt` | `JsonlTracker` writes to local files | Replace with HTTP-based tracker implementation |\n\n### Detailed Refactoring Steps\n\n#### Step 1: Create Thin HTTP Client\n```rust\n// New: src/experiment_tracking_client.rs\npub struct ExperimentTrackingClient {\n    base_url: String,\n    http_client: reqwest::Client,\n}\n\nimpl ExperimentTrackingClient {\n    pub async fn start_run(&self, experiment_id: &str, params: &HyperParams, tags: Vec<String>) -> Result<RunId>;\n    pub async fn log_metrics(&self, experiment_id: &str, run_id: &str, step: usize, metrics: &HashMap<String, f64>) -> Result<()>;\n    pub async fn end_run(&self, experiment_id: &str, run_id: &str, final_metrics: &HashMap<String, f64>) -> Result<()>;\n}\n\n// Implement ExperimentTracker trait for backward compatibility\nimpl ExperimentTracker for ExperimentTrackingClient { /* ... */ }\n```\n\n#### Step 2: Update Configuration\n```toml\n# config.txt changes\n[experiment_tracking]\nenabled = true\nservice_url = \"https://experiment-tracking.internal:8443\"\napi_key_env = \"EXPERIMENT_TRACKING_API_KEY\"\ntimeout_ms = 5000\nretry_attempts = 3\n```\n\n#### Step 3: Modify Consumer Modules\n\n**module_20.txt (HyperparameterRunner)**:\n```rust\n// Before: Vec<Arc<dyn ExperimentTracker>>\n// After: Single HTTP client\npub struct HyperparameterRunner {\n    tuner: Box<dyn Tuner>,\n    bus: Arc<EventBus>,\n    tracking_client: Arc<ExperimentTrackingClient>, // Changed\n}\n```\n\n**module_77.txt (TuningEventHandler observers)**:\n```rust\n// Add new observer that forwards to API\nstruct ApiTrackingObserver {\n    client: Arc<ExperimentTrackingClient>,\n    experiment_id: String,\n    run_id: String,\n}\n\nimpl TuningEventHandler for ApiTrackingObserver {\n    async fn on_event(&self, event: TuningEvent) {\n        match event {\n            TuningEvent::TrialCompleted { trial_id, objective, parameters, .. } => {\n                self.client.log_metrics(&self.experiment_id, &self.run_id, trial_id, &parameters).await;\n            }\n            // ... handle other events\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n#### Step 4: Remove Deprecated Code\n- Delete `FileTracker`, `LocalFileTracker`, `JsonlTracker` implementations\n- Remove SQLite dependencies from `module_71.txt`\n- Remove in-memory `ExperimentRun` storage from `module_12.txt`\n- Remove `TrackerFactory` (replaced by configuration-based client instantiation)\n\n#### Step 5: Update Tests\n- Mock the HTTP client in unit tests\n- Add integration tests that verify API contract\n- Ensure backward compatibility by keeping the `ExperimentTracker` trait interface\n\n## Summary\n\n- **Primary finding**: The experiment tracking functionality is spread across 7 core modules (module_12, module_20, module_25, module_48, module_55, module_71, module_76) that implement variations of the same capability using Strategy, Factory, and Observer patterns.\n\n- **Key components**:\n  - Core types: `ExperimentTracker` (trait), `ExperimentRun`/`ExperimentRecord`, `Trial`/`TrialRecord`, `TrainingEvent`/`TuningEvent`\n  - Persistence: `FileTracker`, `HttpTracker`, `LocalFileTracker`, `JsonlTracker`, SQLite-backed tracker\n  - Factory: `TrackerFactory`, `TunerFactory`\n  - Observer: `ExperimentObserver`, `TrainingObserver`, `TuningObserver`\n\n- **Architectural pattern**: The codebase uses a consistent Strategy + Factory + Observer pattern combination. The new microservice should expose a RESTful API that maps cleanly to the existing `start_run()`, `log_epoch_metrics()`/`log_trial()`, and `end_run()` operations.\n\n- **Recommendations**:\n  1. Create a unified `ExperimentTrackingClient` implementing the existing `ExperimentTracker` trait to minimize consumer code changes\n  2. The microservice should implement the proposed REST API covering experiments, runs, metrics, parameters, artifacts, and tags\n  3. Consumer modules (module_43, module_54, module_76, module_77, module_20) need refactoring to inject the HTTP client instead of local tracker implementations\n  4. Consider maintaining the event bus (`EventBus` from utils.txt) internally but have a dedicated observer that forwards events to the microservice API\n  5. SQLite and file-based persistence should be moved entirely to the microservice, not the monolith\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":331,"cache_read_input_tokens":103494,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":331,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":25,"service_tier":"standard"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"24cdc782-3a25-4d97-93d0-2a8f4dce99a2","uuid":"c68950fe-df47-444d-be98-14ed0a8656c8"}

\"best_accuracy\": 0.912\n  }\n}\n\nResponse 200:\n{\n  \"run_id\": \"run-xyz789\",\n  \"status\": \"completed\",\n  \"end_time\": \"2024-01-15T11:00:00Z\",\n  \"duration_seconds\": 1500\n}\n```\n\n**Query Runs**\n```http\nGET /v1/experiments/exp-abc123/runs?status=completed&sort_by=accuracy&order=desc&limit=10\n\nResponse 200:\n{\n  \"runs\": [\n    {\n      \"run_id\": \"run-xyz789\",\n      \"params\": { \"learning_rate\": 0.001, ... },\n      \"final_metrics\": { \"accuracy\": 0.912, ... },\n      \"duration_seconds\": 1500\n    }\n  ],\n  \"total\": 50,\n  \"page\": 1\n}\n```\n\n## Refactoring Plan\n\n### Modules Requiring Modification\n\n| Module | Current Behavior | Required Change |\n|--------|-----------------|-----------------|\n| `module_12.txt` | Direct in-memory `ExperimentTracker` usage | Replace with HTTP client calls to `POST /runs`, `POST /metrics`, `PATCH /runs/{id}` |\n| `module_20.txt` | `HyperparameterRunner` calls `trackers.log_trial()` directly | Inject `ExperimentTrackingClient` that makes HTTP calls instead of direct trait calls |\n| `module_25.txt` | Contains `FileTracker`, `HttpTracker`, `TrackerFactory` | Remove tracker implementations; keep only a thin HTTP client implementing the `ExperimentTracker` trait |\n| `module_54.txt` | `TuningCoordinator` notifies internal observers | Replace internal observer notifications with API calls to log metrics |\n| `module_55.txt` | `ExperimentObserver` receives `ExperimentEvent` internally | Observer implementations call HTTP endpoints instead of local logging |\n| `module_71.txt` | SQLite-backed `ExperimentTracker` persistence | Remove SQLite code; replace with API client calls |\n| `module_76.txt` | `RetrainTrigger` uses `tracker.record()` | Replace with `POST /experiments/{id}/runs` and metric logging calls |\n| `module_77.txt` | `EventBus.emit()` for `TuningEvent` | Add HTTP client that translates `TuningEvent` to API calls |\n| `module_43.txt` | `ModelMonitor` with internal metric tracking | Metrics ingestion should forward to experiment tracking API |\n| `module_48.txt` | `JsonlTracker` writes to local files | Replace with HTTP-based tracker implementation |\n\n### Detailed Refactoring Steps\n\n#### Step 1: Create Thin HTTP Client\n```rust\n// New: src/experiment_tracking_client.rs\npub struct ExperimentTrackingClient {\n    base_url: String,\n    http_client: reqwest::Client,\n}\n\nimpl ExperimentTrackingClient {\n    pub async fn start_run(&self, experiment_id: &str, params: &HyperParams, tags: Vec<String>) -> Result<RunId>;\n    pub async fn log_metrics(&self, experiment_id: &str, run_id: &str, step: usize, metrics: &HashMap<String, f64>) -> Result<()>;\n    pub async fn end_run(&self, experiment_id: &str, run_id: &str, final_metrics: &HashMap<String, f64>) -> Result<()>;\n}\n\n// Implement ExperimentTracker trait for backward compatibility\nimpl ExperimentTracker for ExperimentTrackingClient { /* ... */ }\n```\n\n#### Step 2: Update Configuration\n```toml\n# config.txt changes\n[experiment_tracking]\nenabled = true\nservice_url = \"https://experiment-tracking.internal:8443\"\napi_key_env = \"EXPERIMENT_TRACKING_API_KEY\"\ntimeout_ms = 5000\nretry_attempts = 3\n```\n\n#### Step 3: Modify Consumer Modules\n\n**module_20.txt (HyperparameterRunner)**:\n```rust\n// Before: Vec<Arc<dyn ExperimentTracker>>\n// After: Single HTTP client\npub struct HyperparameterRunner {\n    tuner: Box<dyn Tuner>,\n    bus: Arc<EventBus>,\n    tracking_client: Arc<ExperimentTrackingClient>, // Changed\n}\n```\n\n**module_77.txt (TuningEventHandler observers)**:\n```rust\n// Add new observer that forwards to API\nstruct ApiTrackingObserver {\n    client: Arc<ExperimentTrackingClient>,\n    experiment_id: String,\n    run_id: String,\n}\n\nimpl TuningEventHandler for ApiTrackingObserver {\n    async fn on_event(&self, event: TuningEvent) {\n        match event {\n            TuningEvent::TrialCompleted { trial_id, objective, parameters, .. } => {\n                self.client.log_metrics(&self.experiment_id, &self.run_id, trial_id, &parameters).await;\n            }\n            // ... handle other events\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n#### Step 4: Remove Deprecated Code\n- Delete `FileTracker`, `LocalFileTracker`, `JsonlTracker` implementations\n- Remove SQLite dependencies from `module_71.txt`\n- Remove in-memory `ExperimentRun` storage from `module_12.txt`\n- Remove `TrackerFactory` (replaced by configuration-based client instantiation)\n\n#### Step 5: Update Tests\n- Mock the HTTP client in unit tests\n- Add integration tests that verify API contract\n- Ensure backward compatibility by keeping the `ExperimentTracker` trait interface\n\n## Summary\n\n- **Primary finding**: The experiment tracking functionality is spread across 7 core modules (module_12, module_20, module_25, module_48, module_55, module_71, module_76) that implement variations of the same capability using Strategy, Factory, and Observer patterns.\n\n- **Key components**:\n  - Core types: `ExperimentTracker` (trait), `ExperimentRun`/`ExperimentRecord`, `Trial`/`TrialRecord`, `TrainingEvent`/`TuningEvent`\n  - Persistence: `FileTracker`, `HttpTracker`, `LocalFileTracker`, `JsonlTracker`, SQLite-backed tracker\n  - Factory: `TrackerFactory`, `TunerFactory`\n  - Observer: `ExperimentObserver`, `TrainingObserver`, `TuningObserver`\n\n- **Architectural pattern**: The codebase uses a consistent Strategy + Factory + Observer pattern combination. The new microservice should expose a RESTful API that maps cleanly to the existing `start_run()`, `log_epoch_metrics()`/`log_trial()`, and `end_run()` operations.\n\n- **Recommendations**:\n  1. Create a unified `ExperimentTrackingClient` implementing the existing `ExperimentTracker` trait to minimize consumer code changes\n  2. The microservice should implement the proposed REST API covering experiments, runs, metrics, parameters, artifacts, and tags\n  3. Consumer modules (module_43, module_54, module_76, module_77, module_20) need refactoring to inject the HTTP client instead of local tracker implementations\n  4. Consider maintaining the event bus (`EventBus` from utils.txt) internally but have a dedicated observer that forwards events to the microservice API\n  5. SQLite and file-based persistence should be moved entirely to the microservice, not the monolith\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}
